# The Immigration (European Economic Area) Regulations 2006

> This law has been repealed or revoked.

2006 No. 1003

IMMIGRATION

The Immigration (European Economic Area) Regulations 2006 (revoked) *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

Made *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

30th March 2006 *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

Laid before Parliament *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

4th April 2006 *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

Coming into force *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

30th April 2006 *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]The Secretary of State, being a Minister designated *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] [^c1546066] for the purposes of section 2(2) of the European Communities Act 1972 *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] [^c1546067] in relation to measures relating to rights of entry into, and residence in, the United Kingdom, in exercise of the powers conferred upon him by that section, and of the powers conferred on him by section 109 of the Nationality, Immigration and Asylum Act 2002 *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] [^c1546068], makes the following Regulations: *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

## PART 1 (affected by repeal, see note) — INTERPRETATION (affected by repeal, see note) ETC (affected by repeal, see note)[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794][^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794][^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

### 1. Citation and commencement (affected by repeal, see note)[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

These Regulations may be cited as the Immigration (European Economic Area) Regulations 2006 and shall come into force on 30 *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]<sup>th *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]</sup> April 2006. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

### 2. General interpretation (affected by repeal, see note)[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(1) In these Regulations— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

- “**the 1971 Act *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]**” means the Immigration Act 1971 *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] [^c1546069];
- “**the 1999 Act *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]**” means the Immigration and Asylum Act 1999 *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] [^c1546070];
- “**the 2002 Act *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]**” means the Nationality, Immigration and Asylum Act 2002; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
- the[^key-84179cfc246c0b4cf96f731b9e5b56fc] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] 2014 Act *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] ” means the Immigration Act 2014; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
- “the[^key-824272a1b0f63fd63c92de5034f0427e] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] Accession Regulations *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] ” means the Accession (Immigration and Worker Registration) Regulations 2004; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
- “civil partner” does not include—[^key-40d0f488fedf8fd8dfc60e3ea75da4e7] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
  - (a) a party to a civil partnership of convenience; or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
  - (b) the civil partner (“C”) of a person (“P”) where a spouse, civil partner or durable partner of C or P is already present in the United Kingdom; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
- “**decision maker *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]**” means the Secretary of State, an immigration officer or an entry clearance officer (as the case may be); *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
- “deportation order” means an order made pursuant to regulation 24(3);[^key-150f42ee947580a1db3fb4d87f1b4214] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
- “derivative residence card” means a card issued to a person, in accordance with regulation 18A, as proof of the holder’s derivative right to reside in the United Kingdom as at the date of issue;[^key-83bb7b2d6a9a5b9a98ebc253c58475a6] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
- “**document certifying permanent residence *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]**” means a document issued to an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] national, in accordance with regulation 18, as proof of the holder's permanent right of residence under regulation 15 as at the date of issue; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
- “durable partner” does not include the durable partner (“D”) of a person (“P”) where a spouse, civil partner or durable partner of D or P is already present in the United Kingdom and where that marriage, civil partnership or durable partnership is subsisting; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
- “[^key-9ffb17d15989c4677ca21ea9c64d2c6a] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] decision” means a decision under these Regulations that concerns— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
  - (a) a person’s entitlement to be admitted to the United Kingdom; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
  - (b) a person’s entitlement to be issued with or have renewed, or not to have revoked, a registration certificate, residence card, derivative residence card, document certifying permanent residence or permanent residence card; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
  - (c) a person’s removal from the United Kingdom; or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
  - (d) the cancellation, pursuant to regulation 20A, of a person’s right to reside in the United Kingdom *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] ;[^key-1a5eb323a2d6ccfab3996ae244151dec] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
- but does not include decisions under regulations 24AA (human rights considerations and interim orders to suspend removal) or 29AA (temporary admission in order to submit case in person) *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794];
- “**EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] family permit *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]**” means a document issued to a person, in accordance with regulation 12, in connection with his admission to the United Kingdom; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
- “**EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] national *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]**” means a national of an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] State *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] who is not also a[^M_F_4e2937eb-cf6a-4818-ed29-d54f91b82816] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] British citizen[^M_F_e5ca7efe-d4ed-434e-c449-474b44ed621a] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794];
- “**EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] State *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]**” means— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
  - (a) a member State, other than the United Kingdom; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
  - (b) Norway, Iceland or Liechtenstein; or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
  - (c) Switzerland; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
- “**entry clearance *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]**” has the meaning given in section 33(1) of the 1971 Act *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] [^c1546072];
- “**entry clearance officer *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]**” means a person responsible for the grant or refusal of entry clearance; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
- “exclusion order” means an order made under regulation 19(1B)[^key-aad860e3db0089c7baae6d3424813875] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794];
- “**immigration rules *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]**” has the meaning given in section 33(1) of the 1971 Act; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
- “**military service *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]**” means service in the armed forces of an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] State; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
- “**permanent residence card *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]**” means a card issued to a person who is not an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] national, in accordance with regulation 18, as proof of the holder's permanent right of residence under regulation 15 as at the date of issue; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
- “[^key-32ee476efa0e7f70a964eb3df46fb651] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794][^key-f09fde77a32543dc8c30db45556724fa]... qualifying *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] State residence card” means— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
  - (a) a valid document[^key-dfee1e72840a3482401d9b86476289c5] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] called a “Residence card of a family member of a Union Citizen” issued under Article 10 of Council *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] [Directive 2004/38/EC *(affected by repeal, see note)*](https://www.legislation.gov.uk/european/regulation/2004/0038)[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] (as applied, where relevant, by the *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] Agreement) by an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] State listed in sub-paragraph (b) to a non- *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] family member of an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] national as proof of the holder’s right of residence in that State; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
  - (b) any[^key-2615f158fe15898a08bb0b714e031843] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] State, except Switzerland *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] ;
- “**registration certificate *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]**” means a certificate issued to an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] national, in accordance with regulation 16, as proof of the holder's right of residence in the United Kingdom as at the date of issue; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
- “**relevant *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] national *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]**” in relation to an extended family member has the meaning given in regulation 8(6); *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
- “**residence card *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]**” means a card issued to a person who is not an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] national, in accordance with regulation 17, as proof of the holder's right of residence in the United Kingdom as at the date of issue; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
- “spouse” does not include—[^key-e2faf9f346eccff692b9bd44b0616f55] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
  - (a) a party to a marriage of convenience; or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
  - (b) the spouse (“S”) of a person (“P”) where a spouse, civil partner or durable partner of S or P is already present in the United Kingdom; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
- [^key-4fd12f1c9e95952b641389ecacaff263]...

(2) Paragraph (1) is subject to paragraph 1(a) of Schedule 4 (transitional provisions). *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(3)[^key-51e9cb25d5d1c1bd521bee5aad75bff3] Section 11 of the 1971 Act (construction of references to entry) shall apply for the purpose of determining whether a person has entered the United Kingdom for the purpose of these Regulations as it applies for the purpose of determining whether a person has entered the United Kingdom for the purpose of that Act. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

### 3. Continuity of residence (affected by repeal, see note)[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(1) This regulation applies for the purpose of calculating periods of continuous residence in the United Kingdom under regulation 5(1) and regulation 15. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(2) Continuity of residence is not affected by — *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

- (a) periods of absence from the United Kingdom which do not exceed six months in total in any year; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
- (b) periods of absence from the United Kingdom on military service; or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
- (c) any one absence from the United Kingdom not exceeding twelve months for an important reason such as pregnancy and childbirth, serious illness, study or vocational training or an overseas posting. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(3) But continuity of residence is broken if a person is removed from the United Kingdom under *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] these Regulations[^key-2a3a493847d70ace7884f4b8eaaa67e9] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794].

### 4. “Worker”, “self-employed person”, “self-sufficient person” and “student” (affected by repeal, see note)[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(1) In these Regulations — *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

- (a) “**worker *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]**” means a worker within the meaning of *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] Article 39 of the Treaty establishing the European Community *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-4e07b27bc98ec214aa5cd08807c41918] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] Article 45 of the Treaty on the Functioning of the European Union *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-4e07b27bc98ec214aa5cd08807c41918] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] [^c1546073];
- (b) “**self-employed person *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]**” means a person who establishes himself in order to pursue activity as a self-employed person in accordance with Article *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] 43 of the Treaty establishing the European Community *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-b8f42bdccfed817be7aba00c2ded49fe] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] Article 49 of the Treaty on the Functioning of the European Union *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-b8f42bdccfed817be7aba00c2ded49fe] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794];
- (c) “**self-sufficient person *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]**” means a person who has— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
  - (i) sufficient resources not to become a burden on the social assistance system of the United Kingdom during his period of residence; and *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
  - (ii (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] comprehensive sickness insurance cover in the United Kingdom; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
- (d) “**student *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]**” means a person who— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
  - (i)[^key-5e156c9783fbd1763c9a0be8aae2b079] is enrolled, for the principal purpose of following a course of study (including vocational training), at a public or private establishment which is— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
    - (aa (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] financed from public funds; or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
    - (bb (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] otherwise recognised by the Secretary of State as an establishment which has been accredited for the purpose of providing such courses or training within the law or administrative practice of the part of the United Kingdom in which the establishment is located; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
  - (ii (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] has comprehensive sickness insurance cover in the United Kingdom; and *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
  - (iii (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] assures the Secretary of State, by means of a declaration, or by such equivalent means as the person may choose, that he has sufficient resources not to become a burden on the social assistance system of the United Kingdom during his period of residence. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(2) For the purposes of paragraph (1)(c) *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] or (d)[^key-163aac24780578774a6088ab99909129] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794], where family members of the person concerned reside in the United Kingdom and their right to reside is dependent upon their being family members of that person— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

- (a) the requirement for that person to have sufficient resources not to become a burden on the social assistance system of the United Kingdom during his period of residence shall only be satisfied if his resources and those of the family members are sufficient to avoid him and the family members becoming such a burden; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
- (b) the requirement for that person to have comprehensive sickness insurance cover in the United Kingdom shall only be satisfied if he and his family members have such cover. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(3)[^key-f48062c142d80baae0282fd403dc30f9] . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . .

(4)[^key-87e97ac7cdbb8c50a9e51cd4ed7e540a] For the purposes of paragraphs (1)(c) and (d) and *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] paragraph (2)[^key-52ed5c92b2a06e4c3ee712d22429a9a6] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794], the resources of the person concerned and, where applicable, any family members, are to be regarded as sufficient if — *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

- (a) they exceed the maximum level of resources which a *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] British citizen[^key-22763afe907fdb5bd55542b57dcbeda3] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] and his family members may possess if he is to become eligible for social assistance under the United Kingdom benefit system; or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
- (b) paragraph (a) does not apply but, taking into account the personal situation of the person concerned and, where applicable, any family members, it appears to the decision maker that the resources of the person or persons concerned should be regarded as sufficient. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(5)[^key-f9fa0da6de9dab6acf960bef751f119f] For the purpose of regulation 15A(2) references in this regulation to “family members” includes a “primary carer” as defined in regulation 15A(7). *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

### 5. “Worker or self-employed person who has ceased activity” (affected by repeal, see note)[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(1) In these Regulations, “ *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]**worker or self-employed person who has ceased activity *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]**” means an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] national who satisfies the conditions in paragraph (2), (3), (4) or (5). *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(2) A person satisfies the conditions in this paragraph if he— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

- (a) terminates his activity as a worker or self-employed person and— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
  - (i) has reached the age at which he is entitled to a state pension on the date on which he terminates his activity; or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
  - (ii (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] in the case of a worker, ceases working to take early retirement; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
- (b) pursued his activity as a worker or self-employed person in the United Kingdom for at least twelve months prior to the termination; and *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
- (c) resided in the United Kingdom continuously for more than three years prior to the termination. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(3) A person satisfies the conditions in this paragraph if— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

- (a) he terminates his activity in the United Kingdom as a worker or self-employed person as a result of a permanent incapacity to work; and *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
- (b) either— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
  - (i) he resided in the United Kingdom continuously for more than two years prior to the termination; or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
  - (ii (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] the incapacity is the result of an accident at work or an occupational disease that entitles him to a pension payable in full or in part by an institution in the United Kingdom. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(4) A person satisfies the conditions in this paragraph if— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

- (a) he is active as a worker or self-employed person in an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] State but retains his place of residence in the United Kingdom, to which he returns as a rule at least once a week; and *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
- (b) prior to becoming so active in that *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] State, he had been continuously resident and continuously active as a worker or self-employed person in the United Kingdom for at least three years. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(5) A person who satisfies the condition in paragraph (4)(a) but not the condition in paragraph (4)(b) shall, for the purposes of paragraphs (2) and (3), be treated as being active and resident in the United Kingdom during any period in which he is working or self-employed in the *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] State. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(6) The conditions in paragraphs (2) and (3) as to length of residence and activity as a worker or self-employed person shall not apply in relation to a person whose spouse or civil partner is a *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] British citizen[^key-152f066b6588350ff79a28b52c496a8f] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794].

(7) Subject to[^key-a21bfdeca55fbc3b9d21291cb3ccb79c] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] “regulations 6(2), 7A(3) or 7B(3)[^key-9907721a28450defd4df67057b04c54a] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794], for the purposes of this regulation— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

- (a) periods of inactivity for reasons not of the person's own making; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
- (b) periods of inactivity due to illness or accident; and *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
- (c) in the case of a worker, periods of involuntary unemployment duly recorded by the relevant employment office, *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

shall be treated as periods of activity as a worker or self-employed person, as the case may be. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

### 6. “Qualified person” (affected by repeal, see note)[^key-db11370bca10af84c13d42fdabab75ed][^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(1)[^key-0bcdf8b1166aaf05211654f57c29aa3e] In these Regulations, “ *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]**qualified person *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]**” means a person who is an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] national and in the United Kingdom as— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

- (a) a jobseeker; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
- (b) a worker; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
- (c) a self-employed person; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
- (d) a self-sufficient person; or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
- (e) a student. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(2)[^key-47fd898239b858760aa0b5ec07d02b4f][^key-f2800b207b47b71b6e9776f682957dde] Subject to[^key-fff30108ea496efad4343490717cc1f7] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] regulations 7A(4) and 7B(4)[^key-0ca581d06a65beb7b1545c99dace1644] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794], a person who is no longer working shall not cease to be treated as a worker for the purpose of paragraph (1)(b) if— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

- (a) he is temporarily unable to work as the result of an illness or accident; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
- (b)[^key-ababc41fe1f0c96460e17e329688566b] he is in duly recorded involuntary unemployment after having been employed in the United Kingdom for at least one year, provided that he— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
  - (i) has registered as a jobseeker with the relevant employment office; and *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
  - (ii (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] satisfies conditions A and B; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
- (ba (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-8fa609c1072fe063338bf0ed201dc9da][^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] he is in duly recorded involuntary unemployment after having been employed in the United Kingdom for less than one year, provided that he— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
  - (i) has registered as a jobseeker with the relevant employment office; and *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
  - (ii (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] satisfies conditions A and B; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
- (c) he is involuntarily unemployed and has embarked on vocational training; or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
- (d) he has voluntarily ceased working and embarked on vocational training that is related to his previous employment. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(2A)[^key-903eaae18c9f71df72d8d979a118c183] A person to whom paragraph (2)(ba) applies may only retain worker status for a maximum of six months. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(3) A person who is no longer in self-employment shall not cease to be treated as a self-employed person for the purpose of paragraph (1)(c) if he is temporarily unable to pursue his activity as a self-employed person as the result of an illness or accident. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(4)[^key-ec8bab1ae3498276d45f58dac1cd2b80] For the purpose of paragraph (1)(a), a “jobseeker” is a person who satisfies conditions *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] A and B *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-1bb9c40dbb6140447f63e3985234e386] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]A, B and, where relevant, C *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-1bb9c40dbb6140447f63e3985234e386] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794].

(5) Condition A is that the person— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

- (a) entered the United Kingdom in order to seek employment; or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
- (b) is present in the United Kingdom seeking employment, immediately after enjoying a right to reside pursuant to paragraph (1)(b) to (e) (disregarding any period during which worker status was retained pursuant to paragraph (2)(b) or (ba)). *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(6) Condition B is that the person can provide evidence that he is seeking employment and has a genuine chance of being engaged. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(7) A person may not retain the status of a worker pursuant to paragraph (2)(b), or jobseeker pursuant to paragraph (1)(a), for longer than *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] six months *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-599ac029388b2d9de4abaf23250c5a0d] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]the relevant period *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-599ac029388b2d9de4abaf23250c5a0d] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] unless he can provide compelling evidence that he is continuing to seek employment and has a genuine chance of being engaged *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794].

(8)[^key-5f2e2041a29d1d4d675de7eaf535d8fe] In paragraph (7), “the relevant period” means— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

- (a) in the case of a person retaining worker status pursuant to paragraph (2)(b), a continuous period of six months; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
- (b) in the case of a jobseeker, *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] 182[^key-f154dfc9843d064fea9bed8a74db571e] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]91 days, minus the cumulative total of any days during which the person concerned previously enjoyed a right to reside as a jobseeker, not including any days prior to a continuous absence from the United Kingdom of at least 12 months. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(9) Condition C applies where the person concerned has, previously, enjoyed a right to reside under this regulation as a result of satisfying conditions A and B— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

- (a) in the case of a person to whom paragraph (2)(b) or (ba) applied, for at least six months; or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
- (b) in the case of a jobseeker, for at least *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] 182[^key-09baaab6d4ff26beceb5fbb803276b70] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]91 days in total, *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

unless the person concerned has, since enjoying the above right to reside, been continuously absent from the United Kingdom for at least 12 months. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(10) Condition C is that the person has had a period of absence from the United Kingdom. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(11) Where condition C applies— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

- (a) paragraph (7) does not apply; and *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
- (b) condition B has effect as if “compelling” were inserted before “evidence”. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

### 7. Family member (affected by repeal, see note)[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(1) Subject to paragraph (2), for the purposes of these Regulations the following persons shall be treated as the family members of another person— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

- (a) his spouse or his civil partner; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
- (b) direct descendants of his, his spouse or his civil partner who are— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
  - (i) under 21; or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
  - (ii (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] dependants of his, his spouse or his civil partner; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
- (c) dependent direct relatives in his ascending line or that of his spouse or his civil partner; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
- (d) a person who is to be treated as the family member of that other person under paragraph (3). *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(2) A person shall not be treated under paragraph (1)(b) or (c) as the family member of a student residing in the United Kingdom after the period of three months beginning on the date on which the student is admitted to the United Kingdom unless— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

- (a) in the case of paragraph (b), the person is the dependent child of the student or of his spouse or civil partner; or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
- (b) the student also falls within one of the other categories of qualified persons mentioned in regulation 6(1). *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(3) Subject to paragraph (4), a person who is an extended family member and has been issued with an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] family permit, a registration certificate or a residence card shall be treated as the family member of the relevant *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] national for as long as he continues to satisfy the conditions in regulation 8(2), (3), (4) or (5) in relation to that *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] national and the permit, certificate or card has not ceased to be valid or been revoked. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(4) Where the relevant *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] national is a student, the extended family member shall only be treated as the family member of that national under paragraph (3) if either the *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] family permit was issued under regulation 12(2), the registration certificate was issued under regulation 16(5) or the residence card was issued under regulation 17(4). *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

### 7A. Application of the Accession Regulations (affected by repeal, see note)[^key-ea06d669c6964f9572d19afb3d0957bd][^key-89f1cd447667f6df05b159c031ddea57][^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(1) This regulation applies to an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] national who was an accession State worker requiring registration on 30th April 2011 (‘an accession worker’). *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(2) In this regulation— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

- “accession State worker requiring registration” has the same meaning as in regulation 1(2)(d) of the Accession Regulations; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
- “legally working” has the same meaning as in regulation 2(7) of the Accession Regulations. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(3) In regulation 5(7)(c), where the worker is an accession worker, periods of involuntary unemployment duly recorded by the relevant employment office shall be treated only as periods of activity as a worker— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

- (a) during any period in which regulation 5(4) of the Accession Regulations applied to that person; or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
- (b) when the unemployment began on or after 1st May 2011. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(4) Regulation 6(2) applies to an accession worker where he— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

- (a) was a person to whom regulation 5(4) of the Accession Regulations applied on 30th April 2011; or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
- (b) became unable to work, became unemployed or ceased to work, as the case maybe, on or after 1st May 2011. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(5) For the purposes of regulation 15, an accession worker shall be treated as having resided in accordance with these Regulations during any period before 1st May 2011 in which the accession worker— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

- (a) was legally working in the United Kingdom; or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
- (b) was a person to whom regulation 5(4) of the Accession Regulations applied. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(6) Subject to paragraph (7), a registration certificate issued to an accession worker under regulation 8 of the Accession Regulations shall, from 1st May 2011, be treated as if it was a registration certificate issued under these Regulations where the accession worker was legally working in the United Kingdom for the employer specified in that certificate on— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

- (a) 30th April 2011; or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
- (b) the date on which the certificate is issued where it is issued after 30th April 2011. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(7) Paragraph (6) does not apply— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

- (a) if the Secretary of State issues a registration certificate in accordance with regulation 16 to an accession worker on or after 1st May 2011; and *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
- (b) from the date of registration stated on that certificate. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

### 7B. Application of the (affected by repeal, see note) EU2 Regulations (affected by repeal, see note)[^key-ea06d669c6964f9572d19afb3d0957bd][^key-1b5a8625190cb62b999c837502a62cf5][^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794][^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(1) This regulation applies to an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] national who was an accession State national subject to worker authorisation before 1st January 2014. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(2) In this regulation— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

- “accession State national subject to worker authorisation” has the same meaning as in regulation 2 of the *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EU2 Regulations *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] ;
- “the *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EU2 Regulations *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] ” means the Accession (Immigration and Worker Authorisation) Regulations 2006. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(3) Regulation 2(12) of the *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EU2 Regulations *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] (accession State national subject to worker authorisation: legally working) has effect for the purposes of this regulation as it does for regulation 2(3) and (4) of the *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EU2 Regulations *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] .

(4) In regulation 5(7)(c), where the worker is an accession State national subject to worker authorisation, periods of involuntary unemployment duly recorded by the relevant employment office must only be treated as periods of activity as a worker when the unemployment began on or after 1st January 2014. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(5) Regulation 6(2) applies to an accession State national subject to worker authorisation where the accession State national subject to worker authorisation became unable to work, became unemployed or ceased to work, as the case may be, on or after 1st January 2014. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(6) For the purposes of regulation 15, an accession State national subject to worker authorisation must be treated as having resided in accordance with these Regulations during any period before 1st January 2014 in which the accession State national subject to worker authorisation was legally working in the United Kingdom. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(7) An accession worker card issued to an accession State national subject to worker authorisation under regulation 11 of the *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EU2 Regulations *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] before 1st January 2014 must be treated as if it were a registration certificate issued under these Regulations so long as it has not expired . *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

### 8. “Extended family member” (affected by repeal, see note)[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(1) In these Regulations “ *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] **extended family member *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]** ” means a person who is not a family member of an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] national under regulation 7(1)(a), (b) or (c) and who satisfies the conditions in paragraph (2), (3), (4) or (5). *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(2) A person satisfies the condition in this paragraph if the person is a relative of an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] national, his spouse or his civil partner and— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

- (a) the person is residing in *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] a country other than the United Kingdom[^key-4a39920930262e4ff4c8b001bae09933] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] [^key-80e36fe15bb04648eca926f8f8ba6db9]... and is dependent upon the *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] national or is a member of his household; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
- (b) the person satisfied the condition in paragraph (a) and is accompanying the *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] national to the United Kingdom or wishes to join him there; or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
- (c) the person satisfied the condition in paragraph (a), has joined the *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] national in the United Kingdom and continues to be dependent upon him or to be a member of his household. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(3) A person satisfies the condition in this paragraph if the person is a relative of an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] national or his spouse or his civil partner and, on serious health grounds, strictly requires the personal care of the *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] national his spouse or his civil partner. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(4) A person satisfies the condition in this paragraph if the person is a relative of an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] national and would meet the requirements in the immigration rules (other than those relating to entry clearance) for indefinite leave to enter or remain in the United Kingdom as a dependent relative of the *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] national were the *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] national a person present and settled in the United Kingdom. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(5) A person satisfies the condition in this paragraph if the person is the partner of an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] national (other than a civil partner) and can prove to the decision maker that he is in a durable relationship with the *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] national.    *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(6) In these Regulations “ *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] **relevant *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] national *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]** ” means, in relation to an extended family member, the *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] national who is or whose spouse or civil partner is the relative of the extended family member for the purpose of paragraph (2), (3) or (4) or the *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] national who is the partner of the extended family member for the purpose of paragraph (5). *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

- (a) the *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^M_F_a4c89b3a-908f-42db-eae3-7149244aa097] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] British citizen[^M_F_1af64496-c090-4108-a3ed-c952c7e7e9ca] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^M_F_a4c89b3a-908f-42db-eae3-7149244aa097] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] is residing in an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^M_F_a4c89b3a-908f-42db-eae3-7149244aa097] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA State *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^M_F_a4c89b3a-908f-42db-eae3-7149244aa097] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] as a worker or self-employed person or was so residing before returning to the United Kingdom; and *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^M_F_a4c89b3a-908f-42db-eae3-7149244aa097] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
- (b) if the family member of the *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^M_F_a4c89b3a-908f-42db-eae3-7149244aa097] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] British citizen[^M_F_afa88dff-5d53-415c-8bc9-c47a03dde531] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^M_F_a4c89b3a-908f-42db-eae3-7149244aa097] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] is his spouse or civil partner, the parties are living together in the *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^M_F_a4c89b3a-908f-42db-eae3-7149244aa097] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA State *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^M_F_a4c89b3a-908f-42db-eae3-7149244aa097] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] or had entered into the marriage or civil partnership and were living together in that State before the *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^M_F_a4c89b3a-908f-42db-eae3-7149244aa097] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] British citizen[^M_F_6e56cae0-8351-4a5e-9fe9-a4f39d5a225d] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^M_F_a4c89b3a-908f-42db-eae3-7149244aa097] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] returned to the United Kingdom. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^M_F_a4c89b3a-908f-42db-eae3-7149244aa097] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(3) Where these Regulations apply to the family member of a *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^M_F_a4c89b3a-908f-42db-eae3-7149244aa097] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] British citizen[^M_F_d89c45f0-2c0a-48a1-fb42-c92268b165fd] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^M_F_a4c89b3a-908f-42db-eae3-7149244aa097] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] the United Kingdom national shall be treated as holding a valid passport issued by an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^M_F_a4c89b3a-908f-42db-eae3-7149244aa097] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA State *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^M_F_a4c89b3a-908f-42db-eae3-7149244aa097] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] for the purpose of the application of regulation 13 to that family member. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^M_F_a4c89b3a-908f-42db-eae3-7149244aa097] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(1) If the conditions in paragraph (2) are satisfied, these Regulations apply to a person who is the family member of a British citizen as if the British citizen (“P”) were an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] national. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(2) The conditions are that— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

- (a) P is residing in an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] State as a worker or self-employed person or was so residing before returning to the United Kingdom; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
- (b) if the family member of P is P’s spouse or civil partner, the parties are living together in the *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] State or had entered into the marriage or civil partnership and were living together in the *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] State before the British citizen returned to the United Kingdom; and *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
- (c) the centre of P’s life has transferred to the *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] State where P resided as a worker or self-employed person. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(3) Factors relevant to whether the centre of P’s life has transferred to another *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] State include— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

- (a) the period of residence in the *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] State as a worker or self-employed person; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
- (b) the location of P’s principal residence; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
- (c) the degree of integration of P in the *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] State. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(4) Where these Regulations apply to the family member of P, P is to be treated as holding a valid passport issued by an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] State for the purpose of the application of regulation 13 to that family member. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

### 10. “Family member who has retained the right of residence” (affected by repeal, see note)[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(1) In these Regulations, “ *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] **family member who has retained the right of residence *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]** ” means, subject to paragraph (8), a person who satisfies the conditions in paragraph (2), (3), (4) or (5). *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(2) A person satisfies the conditions in this paragraph if— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

- (a)[^key-fd9bf135e39b1a5c8080aa4e66e1142d] he was a family member of a qualified person or of an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] national with a permanent right of residence when that person died; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
- (b) he resided in the United Kingdom in accordance with these Regulations for at least the year immediately before the death of *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] the qualified person or the[^key-1695a2c016436cc61de25f370bb8e560] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] national with a permanent right of residence *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] ; and *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
- (c) he satisfies the condition in paragraph (6). *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(3) A person satisfies the conditions in this paragraph if— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

- (a) he is the direct descendant of— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
  - (i) a qualified person or an[^key-a039a6637194bcaafeed9cb6c5c3dd5e] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] national with a permanent right of residence *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] who has died; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
  - (ii (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] a person who ceased to be a qualified person on ceasing to reside in the United Kingdom; or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
  - (iii (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] the person who was the spouse or civil partner of *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] the qualified person or the[^key-2e73fd55b7489ae7f40a3b268385a724] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] national with a permanent right of residence *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] mentioned in sub-paragraph (i) when he died or is the spouse or civil partner of the person mentioned in sub-paragraph (ii); and *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
- (b) he was attending an educational course in the United Kingdom immediately before *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] the qualified person or the[^key-9d9c8389d63118d36409b1fb9b4d6039] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] national with a permanent right of residence *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] died or ceased to be a qualified person and continues to attend such a course. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(4) A person satisfies the conditions in this paragraph if the person is the parent with actual custody of a child who satisfies the condition in paragraph (3). *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(5) A person satisfies the conditions in this paragraph if— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

- (a)[^key-a2b866689acb15bc2fc741d3f8241dea] he ceased to be a family member of a qualified person or of an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] national with a permanent right of residence on the termination of the marriage or civil partnership of that person; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
- (b) he was residing in the United Kingdom in accordance with these Regulations at the date of the termination; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
- (c) he satisfies the condition in paragraph (6); and *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
- (d) either— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
  - (i) prior to the initiation of the proceedings for the termination of the marriage or the civil partnership the marriage or civil partnership had lasted for at least three years and the parties to the marriage or civil partnership had resided in the United Kingdom for at least one year during its duration; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
  - (ii (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] the former spouse or civil partner of *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] the qualified person or the[^key-7bf08982708c0f4054d9e62392e88eed] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] national with a permanent right of residence *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] has custody of a child of the qualified person; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
  - (iii (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-2f711c829e5402427d275aef053a06ea][^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] the former spouse or civil partner of the qualified person or the *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] national with a permanent right of residence has the right of access to a child of the qualified person or the *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] national with a permanent right of residence, where the child is under the age of 18 and where a court has ordered that such access must take place in the United Kingdom; or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
  - (iv (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] the continued right of residence in the United Kingdom of the person is warranted by particularly difficult circumstances, such as he or another family member having been a victim of domestic violence while the marriage or civil partnership was subsisting. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(6) The condition in this paragraph is that the person— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

- (a) is not an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] national but would, if he were an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] national, be a worker, a self-employed person or a self-sufficient person under regulation 6; or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
- (b) is the family member of a person who falls within paragraph (a). *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(7) In this regulation, “ *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] **educational course *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]** ” means a course within the scope of Article 12 of Council Regulation *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] [( EEC *(affected by repeal, see note)* ) No. *(affected by repeal, see note)* 1612/68](https://www.legislation.gov.uk/european/regulation/1968/1612) [^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794][^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] on freedom of movement for workers *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] [^c1546075] .

(8) A person with a permanent right of residence under regulation 15 shall not become a family member who has retained the right of residence on the death or departure from the United Kingdom of *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] the qualified person or the[^key-7e554048b1d2945dfff6040f84aaf5b3] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] national with a permanent right of residence *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] or the termination of the marriage or civil partnership, as the case may be, and a family member who has retained the right of residence shall cease to have that status on acquiring a permanent right of residence under regulation 15. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

## PART 2 (affected by repeal, see note) — EEA (affected by repeal, see note) RIGHTS (affected by repeal, see note)[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794][^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794][^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

### 11. Right of admission to the United Kingdom (affected by repeal, see note)[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(1) An *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] national must be admitted to the United Kingdom if he produces on arrival a valid national identity card or passport issued by an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] State. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(2)[^key-ee6fe998cdbaf6792c07d043970d8047] A person who is not an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] national must be admitted to the United Kingdom if he is— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

- (a) a family member of an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] national and produces on arrival a valid passport and a qualifying *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] State residence card, provided the conditions in regulation 19(2)(a) (non- *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] family member to be accompanying or joining *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] national in the United Kingdom) and (b) ( *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] national must have a right to reside in the United Kingdom under these Regulations) are met; or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
- (b) a family member of an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] national, a family member who has retained the right of residence, a person who meets the criteria in paragraph (5) or a person with a permanent right of residence under regulation 15 and produces on arrival— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
  - (i) a valid passport; and *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
  - (ii (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] family permit, a residence card, a derivative residence card or a permanent residence card. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(3)[^key-233522bdb81517857e2936fddb0b146c] An immigration officer must not place a stamp in the passport of a person admitted to the United Kingdom under this regulation who is not an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] national if the person produces a residence card, a derivative residence card, a permanent residence card or a qualifying *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] State residence card. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(4) Before an immigration officer refuses admission to the United Kingdom to a person under this regulation because the person does not produce on arrival a document mentioned in paragraph (1) or (2), the immigration officer must give the person every reasonable opportunity to obtain the document or have it brought to him within a reasonable period of time or to prove by other means that he is— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

- (a) an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] national; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
- (b) a family member of an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] national with a right to accompany that national or join him in the United Kingdom; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] [^key-5bbcfb1a6b38f9ce233dd92cca62a165] ...
- (c) a family member who has retained the right of residence or a person with a permanent right of residence under regulation 15. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
- (ba (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-b3edaeeac246cb353865a6345da42500][^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] a person who meets the criteria in paragraph (5); or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(5)[^key-855ffc7b71395009102ed58c41d807df] A person (“P”) meets the criteria in this paragraph where— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

- (a) P previously resided in the United Kingdom pursuant to regulation 15A(3) and would be entitled to reside in the United Kingdom pursuant to that regulation were P in the country; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
- (b) P is accompanying an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] national to, or joining an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] national in, the United Kingdom and P would be entitled to reside in the United Kingdom pursuant to regulation 15A(2) were P and the *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] national both in the United Kingdom; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
- (c) P is accompanying a person (“the relevant person”) to, or joining the relevant person in, the United Kingdom and— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
  - (i) the relevant person is residing, or has resided, in the United Kingdom pursuant to regulation 15A(3); and *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
  - (ii (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] P would be entitled to reside in the United Kingdom pursuant to regulation 15A(4) were P and the relevant person both in the *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] United Kingdom;[^key-0783181586c404fedab74e8856ab5734] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
- (d) P is accompanying a person who meets the criteria in (b) or (c) (“the relevant person”) to the United Kingdom and— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
  - (i) P and the relevant person are both— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
    - (aa (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] seeking admission to the United Kingdom in reliance on this paragraph for the first time; or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
    - (bb (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] returning to the United Kingdom having previously resided there pursuant to the same provisions of regulation 15A in reliance on which they now base their claim to admission; and *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
  - (ii (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] P would be entitled to reside in the United Kingdom pursuant to regulation 15A(5) were P and the relevant *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] person there;[^key-1c526f33cac43559fa025308f689f8e0] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
- (e)[^key-fec43f20db148e22dcf313b621eff8c3] P is accompanying a British citizen to, or joining a British citizen in, the United Kingdom and P would be entitled to reside in the United Kingdom pursuant to regulation 15A(4A) were P and the British citizen both in the United Kingdom. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(6) Paragraph (7) applies where— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

- (a) a person (“P”) seeks admission to the United Kingdom in reliance on *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] paragraph (5)(b), (c) or (e)[^key-2cc4a2a2df7a90bdd9fc902ce55d8cb5] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]; and *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
- (b) if P were in the United Kingdom, P would have a derived right of residence by virtue of regulation 15A(7)(b)(ii). *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
