(7) Where this paragraph applies a person (“P”) will only be regarded as meeting the criteria in *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] paragraph (5)(b), (c) or (e)[^key-e98a002f4ac2eff61d8a398f25b70197] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] where P— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

- (a) is accompanying the person with whom P would on admission to the United Kingdom jointly share care responsibility for the purpose of regulation 15A(7)(b)(ii); or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
- (b) has previously resided in the United Kingdom pursuant to *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] regulation 15A(2), (4) or (4A)[^key-03ccb7da2712f4955fcc1a69e20f28b6] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] as a joint primary carer and seeks admission to the United Kingdom in order to reside there again on the same basis. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(8)[^key-621d63cf7c80b94057601416ecd5ba04] But this regulation is subject to regulations 19(1) *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794], (1A)[^key-465b8b9c95a8b3736caa522bfe7b22cc] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794], (1AB) and (2)[^key-e5f0d475a5d11657d17192deddb51011] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] and 23A[^key-193b8362112335b6da760ca9364087d4] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794].

### 12. Issue of (affected by repeal, see note) EEA (affected by repeal, see note) family permit (affected by repeal, see note)[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794][^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794][^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(1) An entry clearance officer must issue an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] family permit to a person who applies for one if the person is a family member of an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] national and— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

- (a) the *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] national— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
  - (i) is residing in the *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] UK *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] in accordance with these Regulations; or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
  - (ii (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] will be travelling to the United Kingdom within six months of the date of the application and will be an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] national residing in the United Kingdom in accordance with these Regulations on arrival in the United Kingdom; and *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
- (b)[^key-2fe83ac074d025af1157ae383780a545] the family member will be accompanying the *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] national to the United Kingdom or joining the *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] national there. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(1A)[^key-5ce3028df2395496ddcfef5b3a08d62e] An entry clearance officer must issue an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] family permit to a person who applies and provides proof that, at the time at which he first intends to use the *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] family permit, he— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

- (a) would be entitled to be admitted to the United Kingdom by virtue of regulation 11(5); and *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
- (b) will (save in the case of a person who would be entitled to be admitted to the United Kingdom by virtue of regulation 11(5)(a)) be accompanying to, or joining in, the United Kingdom any person from whom his right to be admitted to the United Kingdom under regulation 11(5) will be derived. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(1B) An entry clearance officer must issue an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] family permit to a family member who has retained the right of residence. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(2) An entry clearance officer may issue an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] family permit to an extended family member of an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] national who applies for one if— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

- (a) the relevant *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] national satisfies the condition in paragraph (1)(a); *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
- (b) the extended family member wishes to accompany the relevant *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] national to the United Kingdom or to join him there; and *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
- (c) in all the circumstances, it appears to the entry clearance officer appropriate to issue the *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] family permit. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(3) Where an entry clearance officer receives an application under paragraph (2) he shall undertake an extensive examination of the personal circumstances of the applicant and if he refuses the application shall give reasons justifying the refusal unless this is contrary to the interests of national security. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(4) An *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] family permit issued under this regulation shall be issued free of charge and as soon as possible. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(5) But an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] family permit shall not be issued under this regulation if the applicant or the *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] national concerned *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] is not entitled to be admitted to the United Kingdom as a result of regulation 19(1A) or[^key-969bc8d7ecdb384750e8a11bef6662e7] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] (1AB) or[^key-35ebe55f3f14a7c9d03e51b1451950ed] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] falls to be excluded in accordance with regulation 19(1B) *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] .

(6)[^key-168e753158a8f51ddcc131766bce1d1d] An *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] family permit will not be issued under this regulation to a person (“A”) who is the spouse, civil partner or durable partner of a person (“B”) where a spouse, civil partner or durable partner of A or B holds a valid *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] family permit. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

### 13. Initial right of residence (affected by repeal, see note)[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(1) An *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] national is entitled to reside in the United Kingdom for a period not exceeding three months beginning on the date on which he is admitted to the United Kingdom provided that he holds a valid national identity card or passport issued by an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] State. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(2) A family member of an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] national *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] or a family member who has retained the right of residence who is[^key-ab08b566a1ce91a8f4f5ff9cd2609144] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] residing in the United Kingdom under paragraph (1) who is not himself an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] national is entitled to reside in the United Kingdom provided that he holds a valid passport. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(3)[^key-48b9e8dc7a619390241ffcf70233feaa] An *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] national or his family member who becomes an unreasonable burden on the social assistance system of the United Kingdom will cease to have a right to reside under this regulation. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(4) A person who otherwise satisfies the criteria in this regulation will not be entitled to reside in the United Kingdom under this regulation where the Secretary of State *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] or an immigration officer[^key-f4d99ebe22fda6a0b1e0c7dbdb3654fe] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] has made a decision under *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]—[^key-ec7e84fdbd0c735e87c1dbf6ff1af72b] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

- (a) regulation 19(3)(b), 20(1) *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794], 20A(1) or 23A[^key-1ae0cbb2f6d88681022312c100edc267] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]; or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
- (b) regulation 21B(2), where that decision was taken in the preceding twelve months. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

### 14. Extended right of residence (affected by repeal, see note)[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(1) A qualified person is entitled to reside in the United Kingdom for so long as he remains a qualified person. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(2) A family member of a qualified person residing in the United Kingdom under paragraph (1) or of an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] national with a permanent right of residence under regulation 15 is entitled to reside in the United Kingdom for so long as he remains the family member of the qualified person or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] national. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(3) A family member who has retained the right of residence is entitled to reside in the United Kingdom for so long as he remains a family member who has retained the right of residence. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(4) A right to reside under this regulation is in addition to any right a person may have to reside in the United Kingdom under regulation 13 or 15. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(5)[^key-45715d7c75af12f00380fe8191aa861f] A person who otherwise satisfies the criteria in this regulation will not be entitled to a right to reside in the United Kingdom under this regulation where the Secretary of State *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] or an immigration officer[^key-6dd02747aa24f864c556ea86ce96bf47] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] has made a decision under *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] —[^key-c11395d13d4f796d76e88faca092abff] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

- (a) regulation 19(3)(b), 20(1) *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794], 20A(1) or 23A[^key-c00f8396b9aaf1d807ab9cc33bd5da43] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]; or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
- (b) regulation 21B(2) (not including such a decision taken on the basis of regulation 21B(1)(a) or (b)), where that decision was taken in the preceding twelve months. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

### 15. Permanent right of residence (affected by repeal, see note)[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(1) The following persons shall acquire the right to reside in the United Kingdom permanently— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

- (a) an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] national who has resided in the United Kingdom in accordance with these Regulations for a continuous period of five years; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
- (b) a family member of an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] national who is not himself an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] national but who has resided in the United Kingdom with the *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] national in accordance with these Regulations for a continuous period of five years; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
- (c) a worker or self-employed person who has ceased activity; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
- (d) the family member of a worker or self-employed person who has ceased activity; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
- (e) a person who was the family member of a worker or self-employed person where— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
  - (i) the worker or self-employed person has died; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
  - (ii (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] the family member resided with him immediately before his death; and *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
  - (iii (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] the worker or self-employed person had resided continuously in the United Kingdom for at least the two years immediately before his death or the death was the result of an accident at work or an occupational disease; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
- (f) a person who— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
  - (i) has resided in the United Kingdom in accordance with these Regulations for a continuous period of five years; and *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
  - (ii (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] was, at the end of that period, a family member who has retained the right of residence. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(1A)[^key-3536c74cd8daba77ec0e0fb6ca854147] Residence in the United Kingdom as a result of a derivative right of residence does not constitute residence for the purpose of this regulation. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(2) The[^key-046bbf40980582d2f3a068d95d637e2e] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] right of permanent residence under this regulation shall be lost only through absence from the United Kingdom for a period exceeding two consecutive years. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(3)[^key-aebf86ee6865b49820b2481ed61d80c0] A person who satisfies the criteria in this regulation will not be entitled to a permanent right to reside in the United Kingdom where the Secretary of State *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] or an immigration officer[^key-a496b6cd21bc3e39e0d340b812f9a57f] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] has made a decision under *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]—[^key-48b951a8d8aad75d7f2dcfa635384808] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

- (a) regulation 19(3)(b), 20(1) *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794], 20A(1) or 23A[^key-fdb7fddfb658d0bd79a4dbf43e247c62] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]; or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
- (b) regulation 21B(2) (not including such a decision taken on the basis of regulation 21B(1)(a) or (b)), where that decision was taken in the preceding twelve months. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

### 15A. Derivative right of residence (affected by repeal, see note)[^key-65d033127a24657231280642c7e89a4b][^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(1) A person (“P”) who is not *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] an exempt person[^key-76456386555969b4bf8ebbc526a1621f] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] and who satisfies the criteria in paragraph (2), (3), (4) *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794], (4A)[^key-cca7b9a61af55430dcf732f84b86d684] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] or (5) of this regulation is entitled to a derivative right to reside in the United Kingdom for as long as P satisfies the relevant criteria. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(2) P satisfies the criteria in this paragraph if— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

- (a) P is the primary carer of an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] national (“the relevant *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] national”); and *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
- (b) the relevant *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] national— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
  - (i) is under the age of 18; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
  - (ii (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] is residing in the United Kingdom as a self-sufficient person; and *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
  - (iii (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] would be unable to remain in the United Kingdom if P were required to leave. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(3) P satisfies the criteria in this paragraph if— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

- (a) P is the child of an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] national (“the *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] national parent”); *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
- (b) P resided in the United Kingdom at a time when the *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] national parent was residing in the United Kingdom as a worker; and *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
- (c) P is in education in the United Kingdom and was in education there at a time when the *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] national parent was in the United Kingdom. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(4) P satisfies the criteria in this paragraph if— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

- (a) P is the primary carer of a person meeting the criteria in paragraph (3) (“the relevant person”); and *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
- (b) the relevant person would be unable to continue to be educated in the United Kingdom if P were required to leave. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(4A)[^key-d20f69057247adae1d8637e326c535d5] P satisfies the criteria in this paragraph if— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

- (a) P is the primary carer of a British citizen (“the relevant British citizen”); *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
- (b) the relevant British citizen is residing in the United Kingdom; and *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
- (c) the relevant British citizen would be unable to reside in the *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] UK *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] or in another *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] State if P were required to leave. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(5) P satisfies the criteria in this paragraph if— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

- (a) P is under the age of 18; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
- (b) P’s primary carer is entitled to a derivative right to reside in the United Kingdom by virtue of paragraph (2) or (4); *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
- (c) P does not have leave to enter, or remain in, the United Kingdom; and *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
- (d) requiring P to leave the United Kingdom would prevent P’s primary carer from residing in the United Kingdom. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(6) For the purpose of this regulation— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

- (a) “education” excludes nursery education; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] [^key-4f193dd75b740215fd5ac94b4c9550f9] ...
- (b) “worker” does not include a jobseeker or a person who falls to be regarded as a worker by virtue of *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] regulation 6(2); and[^key-c036aa08bbf2b8deee033525a85411c8] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
- (c)[^key-b2399ae655010850a9cdbffbdf36b8ad] “an exempt person” is a person— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
  - (i) who has a right to reside in the United Kingdom as a result of any other provision of these Regulations; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
  - (ii (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] who has a right of abode in the United Kingdom by virtue of section 2 of the 1971 Act; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
  - (iii (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] to whom section 8 of the 1971 Act, or any order made under subsection (2) of that provision, applies; or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
  - (iv (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] who has indefinite leave to enter or remain in the United Kingdom. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(7) P is to be regarded as a “primary carer” of another person if *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

- (a) P is a direct relative or a legal guardian of that person; and *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
- (b) P—
  - (i) is the person who has primary responsibility for that person’s care; or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
  - (ii (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-ebb52180d7cd01a677c6c7fac1a2d77c][^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] shares equally the responsibility for that person’s care with one other person who is not an exempt person. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(7A)[^key-f9fcd8948ef576afe71b400024201290] Where P is to be regarded as a primary carer of another person by virtue of paragraph (7)(b)(ii) the criteria in paragraphs (2)(b)(iii), (4)(b) and (4A)(c) shall be considered on the basis that both P and the person with whom care responsibility is shared would be required to leave the United Kingdom. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(7B) Paragraph (7A) does not apply if the person with whom care responsibility is shared acquired a derivative right to reside in the United Kingdom as a result of this regulation prior to P assuming equal care responsibility. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(8) P will not be regarded as having responsibility for a person’s care for the purpose of paragraph (7) on the sole basis of a financial contribution towards that person’s care. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(9) A person who otherwise satisfies the criteria in paragraph (2), (3), (4) *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794], (4A)[^key-5442b6dd7ea55e2f270319ec4fa53e8f] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] or (5) will not be entitled to a derivative right to reside in the United Kingdom where the Secretary of State *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] or an immigration officer[^key-3748bb880850d28fbedf6ab7bc08b6cd] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] has made a decision under *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]—[^key-7f1c8111d2f133e16669917f06011879] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

- (a) regulation 19(3)(b), 20(1) *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794], 20A(1) or 23A[^key-2d6ccdbef31685b8b1906e9cf845c9fd] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]; or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
- (b) regulation 21B(2), where that decision was taken in the preceding twelve months. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

### 15B. Continuation of a right of residence (affected by repeal, see note)[^key-97ebea872ef3bab5c9de704d94995a4b][^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(1) This regulation applies during any period in which, but for the effect of regulation 13(4), 14(5), 15(3) or 15A(9), a person (“P”) who is in the United Kingdom would be entitled to reside here pursuant to these Regulations. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(2) Where this regulation applies, any right of residence will (notwithstanding the effect of regulation 13(4), 14(5), 15(3) or 15A(9)) be deemed to continue during any period in which— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

- (a) an appeal under regulation 26 could be brought, while P is in the United Kingdom, against a relevant decision (ignoring any possibility of an appeal out of time with permission); or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
- (b) an appeal under regulation 26 against a relevant decision, brought while P is in the United Kingdom, is pending *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] [^key-c74edabb20dd8664487821763e7d65df] ....

(3) Periods during which residence pursuant to regulation 14 is deemed to continue as a result of paragraph (2) will not constitute residence for the purpose of regulation 15 unless and until— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

- (a) a relevant decision is withdrawn by the Secretary of State; or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
- (b) an appeal against a relevant decision is allowed and that appeal is finally determined *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] [^key-4c4ce8239a40e905aec4dff977fd3d66] ....

(4) Periods during which residence is deemed to continue as a result of paragraph (2) will not constitute residence for the purpose of regulation 21(4)(a) unless and until— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

- (a) a relevant decision is withdrawn by the Secretary of State; or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
- (b) an appeal against a relevant decision is allowed and that appeal is finally determined *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] [^key-ca0da2b6e15db28ddb157e1574483bc1] ....

(5) A “relevant decision” for the purpose of this regulation means a decision pursuant to regulation *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] 19(3)(b) or (c)[^key-914310e8b33799d55618d6ad6c25dd2c] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794], 20(1) or 20A(1) which would, but for the effect of paragraph (2), prevent P from residing in the United Kingdom pursuant to these Regulations. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(6)[^key-f0d542710539a112d3e53c1f38954060] This regulation does not affect the ability of the Secretary of State to give directions for P’s removal while an appeal is pending or before it is finally determined. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(7) In this regulation, “pending” and “finally determined” have the meanings given in section 104 of the 2002 Act. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

## PART 3 (affected by repeal, see note) — RESIDENCE DOCUMENTATION (affected by repeal, see note)[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794][^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

### 16. Issue of registration certificate (affected by repeal, see note)[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(1) The Secretary of State must issue a registration certificate to a qualified person immediately on application and production of— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

- (a) a valid identity card or passport issued by an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] State; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
- (b) proof that he is a qualified person. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(2) In the case of a worker, confirmation of the worker's engagement from his employer or a certificate of employment is sufficient proof for the purposes of paragraph (1)(b). *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(3) The Secretary of State must issue a registration certificate to an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] national who is the family member of a qualified person or of an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] national with a permanent right of residence under regulation 15 immediately on application and production of— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

- (a) a valid identity card or passport issued by an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] State; and *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
- (b) proof that the applicant is such a family member. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(4) The Secretary of State must issue a registration certificate to an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] national who is a family member who has retained the right of residence on application and production of— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

- (a) a valid identity card or passport; and *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
- (b) proof that the applicant is a family member who has retained the right of residence. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(5) The Secretary of State may issue a registration certificate to an extended family member not falling within regulation 7(3) who is an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] national on application if— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

- (a) the relevant *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] national in relation to the extended family member is a qualified person or an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] national with a permanent right of residence under regulation 15; and *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
- (b) in all the circumstances it appears to the Secretary of State appropriate to issue the registration certificate. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(6) Where the Secretary of State receives an application under paragraph (5) he shall undertake an extensive examination of the personal circumstances of the applicant and if he refuses the application shall give reasons justifying the refusal unless this is contrary to the interests of national security. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(7) A registration certificate issued under this regulation shall state the name and address of the person registering and the date of registration *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] [^key-29cabed9bc5f51b7f562ca13015b355c] ....

(8)[^key-bdb3dccdbf88da8e0bf1231d5b2dd267] But this regulation is subject to regulations 7A(6) and 20(1). *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

### 17. Issue of residence card (affected by repeal, see note)[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(1) The Secretary of State must issue a residence card to a person who is not an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] national and is the family member of a qualified person or of an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] national with a permanent right of residence under regulation 15 on application and production of— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

- (a) a valid passport; and *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
- (b) proof that the applicant is such a family member. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(2) The Secretary of State must issue a residence card to a person who is not an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] national but who is a family member who has retained the right of residence on application and production of— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

- (a) a valid passport; and *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
- (b) proof that the applicant is a family member who has retained the right of residence. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(3) On receipt of an application under paragraph (1) or (2) and the documents that are required to accompany the application the Secretary of State shall immediately issue the applicant with a certificate of application for the residence card and the residence card shall be issued no later than six months after the date on which the application and documents are received. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(4) The Secretary of State may issue a residence card to an extended family member not falling within regulation 7(3) who is not an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] national on application if— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

- (a) the relevant *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] national in relation to the extended family member is a qualified person or an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] national with a permanent right of residence under regulation 15; and *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
- (b) in all the circumstances it appears to the Secretary of State appropriate to issue the residence card. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(5) Where the Secretary of State receives an application under paragraph (4) he shall undertake an extensive examination of the personal circumstances of the applicant and if he refuses the application shall give reasons justifying the refusal unless this is contrary to the interests of national security. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(6)[^key-ece7d2c33f9dbeba97107f9258fee208] A residence card issued under this regulation may take the form of a stamp in the applicant's passport and shall be *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] [^key-96e0dbfbfabbae670f75ee719cdef150] ... valid for— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

- (a) five years from the date of issue; or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
- (b) in the case of a residence card issued to the family member or extended family member of a qualified person, the envisaged period of residence in the United Kingdom of the qualified person, *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

whichever is the shorter. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(6A)[^key-85a68bc6cd0083af66ae5795fd83392e] A residence card issued under this regulation shall be entitled “Residence card of a family member of an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] national” or “Residence card of a family member who has retained the right of residence”, as the case may be. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(7)[^key-336acaa6d8f8981a30cceb6b811266a3] . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . .

(8) But this regulation is subject to *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] regulations 20(1) and (1A)[^key-8e6052b68395c7cd2a9e4cd5492aa03f] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794].

### 18. Issue of a document certifying permanent residence and a permanent residence card (affected by repeal, see note)[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(1) The Secretary of State must issue an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] national with a permanent right of residence under regulation 15 with a document certifying permanent residence as soon as possible after an application for such a document and proof that the *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] national has such a right is submitted to the Secretary of State. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(2) The Secretary of State must issue a person who is not an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] national who has a permanent right of residence under regulation 15 with a permanent residence card no later than six months after the date on which an application for a permanent residence card and proof that the person has such a right is submitted to the Secretary of State. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(3) Subject to paragraph (5) *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] [^key-a5d85d26ae76a8262ddd01f240a14a7f] ..., a permanent residence card shall be valid for ten years from the date of issue and must be renewed on application. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(4)[^key-2fc5f56115aab9e28ef3d4d23fc65d3d] . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . .

(5) A document certifying permanent residence and a permanent residence card shall cease to be valid if the holder ceases to have a right of permanent residence under regulation 15. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(6)[^key-d4583dfc715a28b75f1229cfc48c9d66] But this regulation is subject to regulation 20. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

### 18A. Issue of a derivative residence card (affected by repeal, see note)[^key-a16217e48fe7ffda677aa62d897b2578][^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(1) The Secretary of State must issue a person with a derivative residence card on application and on production of— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

- (a) a valid identity card issued by an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] State or a valid passport; and *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
- (b) proof that the applicant has a derivative right of residence under regulation 15A. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(2) On receipt of an application under paragraph (1) the Secretary of State must issue the applicant with a certificate of application as soon as possible. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(3) A derivative residence card issued under paragraph (1) may take the form of a stamp in the applicant’s passport and will be valid until— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

- (a) a date five years from the date of issue; or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
- (b) any other date specified by the Secretary of State when issuing the derivative residence card. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(4) A derivative residence card issued under paragraph (1) must be issued *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] [^key-f34b18304a3e9be1480606bc613b1753] ...as soon as practicable. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(5) But this regulation is subject to regulations 20(1) and 20(1A). *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

## PART 4 (affected by repeal, see note) — REFUSAL OF ADMISSION AND REMOVAL (affected by repeal, see note) ETC (affected by repeal, see note)[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794][^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794][^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

### 19. Exclusion and removal from the United Kingdom (affected by repeal, see note)[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(1) A person is not entitled to be admitted to the United Kingdom by virtue of regulation 11 if his exclusion is justified on grounds of public policy, public security or public health in accordance with regulation 21. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(1A)[^key-1e1345f576b13c2f7e8d3f99156db938] A person is not entitled to be admitted to the United Kingdom by virtue of regulation 11 if that person is subject to a deportation or exclusion order *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794], except where the person is temporarily admitted pursuant to regulation 29AA[^key-eafb6cdc85385904e61108fddd3a321c] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794].

(1AB (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-43385bcfe79c2b5673bf31a615acbf3d][^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] A person is not entitled to be admitted to the United Kingdom by virtue of regulation 11 if the Secretary of State considers there to be reasonable grounds to suspect that his admission would lead to the abuse of a right to reside in accordance with regulation 21B(1). *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(1B) If the Secretary of State considers that the exclusion of an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] national or the family member of an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] national is justified on the grounds of public policy, public security or public health in accordance with regulation 21 the Secretary of State may make an order for the purpose of these Regulations prohibiting that person from entering the United Kingdom. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(2) A person is not entitled to be admitted to the United Kingdom as the family member of an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] national under regulation 11(2) unless, at the time of his arrival— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

- (a) he is accompanying the *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] national or joining him in the United Kingdom; and *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
- (b) the *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] national has a right to reside in the United Kingdom under these Regulations. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(3)[^key-74b5c361905b78e5e8ffe279f607768d] Subject to paragraphs (4) and (5), an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] national who has entered the United Kingdom or the family member of such a national who has entered the United Kingdom may be removed if – *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

- (a)[^key-2d0b8b307210e3fd1a56db9ca16bc028] that person does not have or ceases to have a right to reside under these Regulations; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
- (b) the Secretary of State has decided that the person’s removal is justified on grounds of public policy, public security or public health in accordance with regulation 21; or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
- (c) the Secretary of State has decided that the person’s removal is justified on grounds of abuse of rights in accordance with regulation 21B(2). *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(4) A person must not be removed under paragraph (3) as the automatic consequence of having recourse to the social assistance system of the United Kingdom. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(5) A person must not be removed under paragraph (3) if he has a right to remain in the United Kingdom by virtue of leave granted under the 1971 Act unless his removal is justified on the grounds of public policy, public security or public health in accordance with regulation 21. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

### 20. Refusal to issue or renew and revocation of residence documentation (affected by repeal, see note)[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(1) The Secretary of State may refuse to issue, revoke or refuse to renew a registration certificate, a residence card, a document certifying permanent residence or a permanent residence card if the refusal or revocation is justified on grounds of public policy, public security or public health *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] or on grounds of abuse of rights in accordance with regulation 21B(2)[^key-7a3e51508be301aeec3ea4a55903b3ff] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794].

(1A)[^key-e08ef2e1968b932bac80de55fba4db97] A decision under regulation 19(3)[^key-932b6d0a8cdc98985b45a02e641164b5] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] or 24(4)[^key-8509db80334dd0b0b6c1b8c5f8ec4f45] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] to remove a person from the United Kingdom *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] , or a decision under regulation 23A to revoke a person’s admission to the United Kingdom,[^key-2c13a74c3c7699d2e5344246ff236633] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] will (save during any period in which a right of residence is deemed to continue as a result of regulation 15B(2)) invalidate *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] a registration certificate, residence card, document certifying permanent residence or permanent residence card held by that person or an application made by that person for such a certificate, card or document. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(2) The Secretary of State may revoke a registration certificate or a residence card or refuse to renew a residence card if the holder of the certificate or card has ceased to have *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] , or never had,[^key-c51fe327781c974211c5332b038de9d5] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] a right to reside under these Regulations. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(3) The Secretary of State may revoke a document certifying permanent residence or a permanent residence card or refuse to renew a permanent residence card if the holder of the certificate or card has ceased to have *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] , or never had,[^key-c187246e661a07b46cd790d371db982d] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] a right of permanent residence under regulation 15. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(4) An immigration officer may, at the time of a person's arrival in the United Kingdom— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

- (a) revoke that person's residence card if he is not at that time the family member of a qualified person or of an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] national who has a right of permanent residence under regulation 15, a family member who has retained the right of residence or a person with a right of permanent residence under regulation 15; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
- (b) revoke that person's permanent residence card if he is not at that time a person with a right of permanent residence under regulation 15. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(5) An entry clearance officer or immigration officer may at any time revoke a person’s[^key-451b292c6922b7e4d84898a7a940ad75] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] family permit if— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

- (a) the revocation is justified on grounds of public policy, public security or public health; or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
- (b) the person is not at that time the family member of an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] national with the right to reside in the United Kingdom under these Regulations or is not accompanying that national or joining him in the United Kingdom. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(6) Any action taken under this regulation on grounds of public policy, public security or public health shall be in accordance with regulation 21. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

### 20A. Cancellation of a right of residence (affected by repeal, see note)[^key-7d852344040be95dcdf55a5b5342230f][^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(1) Where the conditions in paragraph (2) are met the Secretary of State may cancel a person’s right to reside in the United Kingdom pursuant to these Regulations. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(2) The conditions in this paragraph are met where— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

- (a) a person has a right to reside in the United Kingdom as a result of these Regulations; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
- (b) the Secretary of State has decided that the cancellation of that person’s right to reside in the United Kingdom is justified on grounds of public policy, public security or public health in accordance with regulation 21 *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] or on grounds of abuse of rights in accordance with regulation 21B(2)[^key-cfe7320317f7c76c987dad23a10a1f33] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794];
- (c) the circumstances are such that the Secretary of State cannot make a decision under regulation 20(1); and *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
- (d) it is not possible for the Secretary of State to remove the person from the United Kingdom pursuant to regulation 19(3)(b) *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] or (c)[^key-7b1fd8f628a6ec05c54b45999ee389f3] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794].

### 20B. Verification of a right of residence (affected by repeal, see note)[^key-861353c9c94da1b562d3609490972b89][^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(1) This regulation applies when the Secretary of State— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

- (a) has reasonable doubt as to whether a person (“A”) has a right to reside under regulation 14(1) or (2); or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
- (b) wants to verify the eligibility of a person (“A”) to apply for documentation issued under Part 3. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(2) The Secretary of State may invite A to— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

- (a) provide evidence to support the existence of a right to reside, or to support an application for documentation under Part 3; or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
- (b) attend an interview with the Secretary of State. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(3) If A purports to be entitled to a right to reside on the basis of a relationship with another person (“B”), the Secretary of State may invite B to— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

- (a) provide information about their relationship with A; or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
- (b) attend an interview with the Secretary of State. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(4) If, without good reason, A or B fail to provide the additional information requested or, on at least two occasions, fail to attend an interview if so invited, the Secretary of State may draw any factual inferences about A’s entitlement to a right to reside as appear appropriate in the circumstances. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(5) The Secretary of State may decide following an inference under paragraph (4) that A does not have or ceases to have a right to reside. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(6) But the Secretary of State must not decide that A does not have or ceases to have a right to reside on the sole basis that A failed to comply with this regulation. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(7) This regulation may not be invoked systematically. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(8) In this regulation, “a right to reside” means a right to reside under these Regulations. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

### 21. Decisions taken on public policy, public security and public health grounds (affected by repeal, see note)[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(1) In this regulation a “ *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] **relevant decision *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]** ” means an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] decision taken on the grounds of public policy, public security or public health. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(2) A relevant decision may not be taken to serve economic ends. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(3) A relevant decision may not be taken in respect of a person with a permanent right of residence under regulation 15 except on serious grounds of public policy or public security. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(4) A relevant decision may not be taken except on imperative grounds of public security in respect of an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] national who— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

- (a) has resided in the United Kingdom for a continuous period of at least ten years prior to the relevant decision; or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
- (b) is under the age of 18, unless the relevant decision is necessary in his best interests, as provided for in the Convention on the Rights of the Child adopted by the General Assembly of the United Nations on 20th November 1989 *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] [^c1546076] .

(5) Where a relevant decision is taken on grounds of public policy or public security it shall, in addition to complying with the preceding paragraphs of this regulation, be taken in accordance with the following principles— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

- (a) the decision must comply with the principle of proportionality; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
- (b) the decision must be based exclusively on the personal conduct of the person concerned; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
- (c) the personal conduct of the person concerned must represent a genuine, present and sufficiently serious threat affecting one of the fundamental interests of society; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
- (d) matters isolated from the particulars of the case or which relate to considerations of general prevention do not justify the decision; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
- (e) a person's previous criminal convictions do not in themselves justify the decision. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(6) Before taking a relevant decision on the grounds of public policy or public security in relation to a person who is resident in the United Kingdom the decision maker must take account of considerations such as the age, state of health, family and economic situation of the person, the person's length of residence in the United Kingdom, the person's social and cultural integration into the United Kingdom and the extent of the person's links with his country of origin. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(7) In the case of a relevant decision taken on grounds of public health— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

- (a) a disease that does not have epidemic potential as defined by the relevant instruments of the World Health Organisation *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] [^c1546077] or is not a disease *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] listed in Schedule 1 to the Health Protection (Notification) Regulations 2010[^key-f528b2e9677bbc9477be3636873b7c7f] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] shall not constitute grounds for the decision; and *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
- (b) if the person concerned is in the United Kingdom, diseases occurring after the three month period beginning on the date on which he arrived in the United Kingdom shall not constitute grounds for the decision. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

### 21A. Application of Part 4 to persons with a derivative right of residence (affected by repeal, see note)[^key-9473af02dc04c9c18bf5146cacbcca89][^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(1) Where this regulation applies Part 4 of these Regulations applies subject to the modifications listed in paragraph (3). *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(2) This regulation applies where a person— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

- (a) would, notwithstanding Part 4 of these Regulations, have a right to be admitted to, or reside in, the United Kingdom by virtue of a derivative right of residence arising under regulation 15A(2), (4) *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794], (4A)[^key-9a9e99a97a4e08295f9fc5a5862ffcc1] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] or (5); *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
- (b) holds a derivative residence card; or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
- (c) has applied for a derivative residence card. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(3) Where this regulation applies Part 4 applies in relation to the matters listed in paragraph (2) as if— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

- (a) references to a matter being *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] [^key-089381e39b3e47726d938971db70665c] ...justified on grounds of public policy, public security or public health in accordance with regulation 21 *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] [^key-089381e39b3e47726d938971db70665c] ... referred instead to a matter being “conducive to the public good”; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
- (b) the reference in regulation 20(5)(a) to a matter being “justified on grounds of public policy, public security or public health” referred instead to a matter being “conducive to the public good”; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
- (c) references to “the family member of an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] national” referred instead to “a person with a derivative right of residence”; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
- (d) references to “a registration certificate, a residence card, a document certifying permanent residence or a permanent residence card” referred instead to “a derivative residence card”; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
- (e) the reference in regulation 19(1A) to a deportation or exclusion order referred also to a deportation or exclusion order made under any provision of the immigration Acts. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
- (f) regulation 20(4) instead conferred on an immigration officer the power to revoke a derivative residence card where the holder is not at that time a person with a derivative right of residence; and *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
- (g) regulations 20(3), 20(6) and 21 were omitted. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

### 21B. Abuse of rights or fraud (affected by repeal, see note)[^key-2f95a733cd72eae46a15e561dcad495c][^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(1) The abuse of a right to reside includes— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

- (a) engaging in conduct which appears to be intended to circumvent the requirement to be a qualified person; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
- (b) attempting to enter the United Kingdom within 12 months of being removed pursuant to regulation 19(3)(a), where the person attempting to do so is unable to provide evidence that, upon re-entry to the United Kingdom, the conditions for any right to reside, other than the initial right of residence under regulation 13, will be met; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
- (c) entering, attempting to enter or assisting another person to enter or attempt to enter, a marriage or civil partnership of convenience; or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
- (d) fraudulently obtaining or attempting to obtain, or assisting another to obtain or attempt to obtain, a right to reside. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(2) The Secretary of State may take an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] decision on the grounds of abuse of rights where there are reasonable grounds to suspect the abuse of a right to reside and it is proportionate to do so. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(3) Where these Regulations provide that an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] decision taken on the grounds of abuse in the preceding twelve months affects a person’s right to reside, the person who is the subject of that decision may apply to the Secretary of State to have the effect of that decision set aside on grounds that there has been a material change in the circumstances which justified that decision. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(4) An application under paragraph (3) may only be made whilst the applicant is outside the United Kingdom. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(5) This regulation may not be invoked systematically. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(6) In this regulation, “a right to reside” means a right to reside under these Regulations. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

## PART 5 (affected by repeal, see note) — PROCEDURE IN RELATION TO (affected by repeal, see note) EEA (affected by repeal, see note) DECISIONS (affected by repeal, see note)[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794][^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794][^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794][^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

### 22. Person claiming right of admission (affected by repeal, see note)[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(1) This regulation applies to a person who claims a right of admission to the United Kingdom under regulation 11 as— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

- (a)[^key-fdbf5d3042b0b92c8180d9e4de28eb99] a person, not being an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] national, who— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
  - (i) is a family member of an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] national; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
  - (ii (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] is a family member who has retained the right of residence; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
  - (iii (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] has a derivative right of residence; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
  - (iv (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] has a permanent right of residence under regulation 15; or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
  - (v) is in possession of a qualifying *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] State residence card; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-6eddca2c1977859457c4db44558d2be6] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
- (b)[^key-6973e77df10a5aa2dbc9e6a9ae0e5a5b] an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] national, where there is reason to believe that he may fall to be excluded under regulation 19(1) *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] (1A) or (1AB)[^key-6983040cf0dcf2eb5c00f282bec079cd] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]; or[^key-b24088dcdc56ad69dd12bf5b4cace5bb] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
- (c)[^key-034c48bb1d7e4adbd835a07bb963096a] a person to whom regulation 29AA applies *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(2) A person to whom this regulation applies is to be treated as if he were a person seeking leave to enter the United Kingdom under the 1971 Act for the purposes of paragraphs 2, 3, 4, 7, 16 to *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] 18A[^M_F_d0405d96-0cad-40aa-9916-5c8d6cb6a3f3] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] and 21 to 24 of Schedule 2 to the 1971 Act *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] [^c1546079] (administrative provisions as to control on entry *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] etc *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]), except that— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

- (a) the reference in paragraph 2(1) to the purpose for which the immigration officer may examine any persons who have arrived in the United Kingdom is to be read as a reference to the purpose of determining whether he is a person who is to be granted admission under these Regulations; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
- (b) the references in paragraphs 4(2A), 7 and 16(1) to a person who is, or may be, given leave to enter are to be read as references to a person who is, or may be, granted admission under these Regulations; and *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
- (c) a medical examination is not be carried out under paragraph 2 or paragraph 7 as a matter of routine and may only be carried out within three months of a person's arrival in the United Kingdom. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(3) For so long as a person to whom this regulation applies is detained, or temporarily admitted or released while liable to detention, under the powers conferred by Schedule 2 to the 1971 Act, he is deemed not to have been admitted to the United Kingdom. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

### 23. Person refused admission (affected by repeal, see note)[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(1) This regulation applies to a person who is in the United Kingdom and has been refused admission to the United Kingdom— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

- (a) because he does not meet the requirement of regulation 11 (including where he does not meet those requirements because his *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] family permit, residence card *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] , derivative residence card[^key-0e36e5e03a4e51c21df0ab569e44f2de] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] or permanent residence card has been revoked by an immigration officer in accordance with regulation 20); or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
- (b) in accordance with regulation *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] 19(1), (1A)[^key-5355f1ec3956eef87d0d2e6e14f21329] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794], (1AB)[^key-22a09bcd5737c9325ce78b4b5f3f7d2f] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] or (2) *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794].
