(2) A person to whom this regulation applies, is to be treated as if he were a person refused leave to enter under the 1971 Act for the purpose of paragraphs 8, 10, 10A, 11, 16 to 19 and 21 to 24 of Schedule 2 to the 1971 Act, except that the reference in paragraph 19 to a certificate of entitlement, entry clearance or work permit is to be read as a reference to an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] family permit, residence card *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] , derivative residence card[^key-978a68e7417aadf8c8cec3e701bf27d5] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] , a qualifying[^key-280d15590203cedc983dcf027d6d5cdc] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] State residence card, or a permanent residence card *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794].

### 23A. Revocation of admission (affected by repeal, see note)[^key-30c020353839bc9be611363328b3d816][^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(1) This regulation applies to a person admitted to the United Kingdom under regulation 11 in circumstances where, pursuant to regulation 19(1) (exclusion justified on grounds of public policy, public security or public health), (1A) (person subject to deportation order or exclusion order) or (1AB) (reasonable grounds to suspect that admission would lead to the abuse of a right to reside), that person was not entitled to be admitted. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(2) Paragraph 6(2) of Schedule 2 to the 1971 Act (administrative provisions as to control on entry: refusal of leave to enter) applies to a person to whom this regulation applies, as though the references— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

- (a) to that person’s examination under paragraph 2 of Schedule 2 to the 1971 Act were to that paragraph as applied by regulation 22(2)(a) and (c) of these Regulations; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
- (b) to notices of leave to enter the United Kingdom were to a decision to admit that person to the United Kingdom under these Regulations; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
- (c) to the cancellation of such a notice and the refusal of leave to enter were to revocation of the decision to admit that person to the United Kingdom under this regulation. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(3) Where a person’s admission to the United Kingdom is revoked, that person is to be treated as a person to whom admission to the United Kingdom has been refused and regulation 23 applies accordingly. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

### 24. Person subject to removal (affected by repeal, see note)[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(1)[^key-b2a6b65d56d90c45072b97be7a2fbd24] If there are reasonable grounds for suspecting that a person is someone who may be removed from the United Kingdom under *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] regulation 19(3)(b)” and for “an immigration officer” substitute “the Secretary of State[^key-b46057fe1519b0506d672780392ed2d2] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794], that person may be detained under the authority of an immigration officer pending a decision whether or not to remove the person under that regulation, and paragraphs 17 and 18 of Schedule 2 to the 1971 Act shall apply in relation to the detention of such a person as those paragraphs apply in relation to a person who may be detained under paragraph 16 of that Schedule. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(2) Where a decision is taken to remove a person[^key-64bc28ce204c693586e65a85f63e2584] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] under regulation 19(3)(a), *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] or (c)[^key-9b80a4f281de646fe277be2d403c282e] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] the person is to be treated as if he were a person to whom section 10(1)(a) of the 1999 Act *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] [^c1546080] applied, and section 10 of that Act (removal of certain persons unlawfully in the United Kingdom) is to apply accordingly. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(3) Where a decision is taken to remove a person *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] under regulation 19(3)(b), the person is to be treated as if he were a person to whom section 3(5)(a) of the 1971 Act *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] [^c1546081] (liability to deportation) applied, and section 5 of that Act *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] [^c1546082] (procedure for deportation) and Schedule 3 to that Act *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] [^c1546083] (supplementary provision as to deportation) are to apply accordingly. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(4)[^key-000ccbfd00343a48f2b3700f6af844cb] A person who enters the United Kingdom in breach of a deportation or exclusion order *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] , or in circumstances where that person was not entitled to be admitted pursuant to regulation 19(1) or (1AB),[^key-6ef21f674f8c59a2365e5da0e17bc81b] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] shall be removable as an illegal entrant under Schedule 2 to the 1971 Act and the provisions of that Schedule shall apply accordingly. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(5) Where such a deportation order is made against a person but he is not removed under the order during the two year period beginning on the date on which the order is made, the Secretary of State shall only take action to remove the person under the order after the end of that period if, having assessed whether there has been any material change in circumstances since the deportation order was made, he considers that the removal continues to be justified on the grounds of public policy, public security or public health. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(6) A person to whom this regulation applies shall be allowed one month to leave the United Kingdom, beginning on the date on which he is notified of the decision to remove him, before being removed pursuant to that decision except— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

- (a) in duly substantiated cases of urgency; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
- (b) where the person is detained pursuant to the sentence or order of any court; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
- (c) where a person is a person to whom regulation 24(4) applies. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(7)[^key-ba46ab0f7e7d263b2e9635e1309d5a8b] Paragraph (6) of this regulation does not apply where a decision has been taken under regulation 19(3) on the basis that the relevant person— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

- (a) has ceased to have a derivative right of residence; or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
- (b) is a person who would have had a derivative right of residence but for the effect of a decision to remove under regulation 19(3)(b). *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

### 24A. Revocation of deportation and exclusion orders (affected by repeal, see note)[^key-934cdb8238784a33a2577aba9318dc9c][^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(1) A deportation or exclusion order shall remain in force unless it is revoked by the Secretary of State under this regulation. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(2) A person who is subject to a deportation or exclusion order may apply to the Secretary of State to have it revoked if the person considers that there has been a material change in the circumstances that justified the making of the order. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(3) An application under paragraph (2) shall set out the material change in circumstances relied upon by the applicant and may only be made whilst the applicant is outside the United Kingdom. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(4) On receipt of an application under paragraph (2), the Secretary of State shall revoke the order if the Secretary of State considers that *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] the criteria for making such an order are no longer satisfied[^key-917fe26dceb52af848510915b90f4876] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794].

(5) The Secretary of State shall take a decision on an application under paragraph (2) no later than six months after the date on which the application is received. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

### 24AA (affected by repeal, see note). Human rights considerations and interim orders to suspend removal (affected by repeal, see note)[^key-dc3dec9ed71cb80630328d422cab0ff6][^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794][^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(1) This regulation applies where the Secretary of State intends to give directions for the removal of a person (“P”) to whom regulation 24(3) applies, in circumstances where— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

- (a) P has not appealed against the *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] decision to which regulation 24(3) applies, but would be entitled, and remains within time, to do so from within the United Kingdom (ignoring any possibility of an appeal out of time with permission); or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
- (b) P has so appealed but the appeal has not been finally determined. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(2) The Secretary of State may only give directions for P’s removal if the Secretary of State certifies that, despite the appeals process not having been begun or not having been finally determined, removal of P to the country or territory to which P is proposed to be removed, pending the outcome of P’s appeal, would not be unlawful under section 6 of the Human Rights Act 1998 (public authority not to act contrary to Human Rights Convention). *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(3) The grounds upon which the Secretary of State may certify a removal under paragraph (2) include (in particular) that P would not, before the appeal is finally determined, face a real risk of serious irreversible harm if removed to the country or territory to which P is proposed to be removed. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(4) If P applies to the appropriate court or tribunal (whether by means of judicial review or otherwise) for an interim order to suspend enforcement of the removal decision, P may not be removed from the United Kingdom until such time as the decision on the interim order has been taken, except— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

- (a) where the expulsion decision is based on a previous judicial decision; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
- (b) where P has had previous access to judicial review; or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
- (c) where the removal decision is based on imperative grounds of public security. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(5) In this regulation, “finally determined” has the same meaning as in Part 6. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

## PART 6 (affected by repeal, see note) — APPEALS UNDER THESE REGULATIONS (affected by repeal, see note)[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794][^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

### 25. Interpretation of Part 6 (affected by repeal, see note)[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(1) In this Part— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

- [^key-3964e7775c7b87ce56f68c36936e0cc8]...
- “Asylum claim” has the meaning given in section 113(1) of the 2002 Act; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-6bb3d5c3fa4c66de9bb5eeb0045b7dac] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
- “**Commission *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]**” has the same meaning as in the Special Immigration Appeals Commission Act 1997 *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] [^c1546084];
- [^key-e163289e813c0ca404bb8b5ec47b9d44].........
- “Human rights claim” has the meaning given in section 113(1) of the 2002 Act. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-6bb3d5c3fa4c66de9bb5eeb0045b7dac] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
- [^key-e163289e813c0ca404bb8b5ec47b9d44].........

(2) For the purposes of this Part, and subject to paragraphs (3) and (4), an appeal is to be treated as pending during the period when notice of appeal is given and ending when the appeal is finally determined, withdrawn or abandoned. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(3) An appeal is not to be treated as finally determined while a further appeal may be brought; and, if such a further appeal is brought, the original appeal is not to be treated as finally determined until the further appeal is determined, withdrawn or abandoned. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(4) A pending appeal is not to be treated as abandoned solely because the appellant leaves the United Kingdom. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

### 26. Appeal rights (affected by repeal, see note)[^key-3c916a979631e3da0234c754d6929d90][^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(3)[^key-4c62a8a7a52b82301b118806a0144a81] If a person *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] to whom paragraph (2) does not apply[^key-3908c730d67c2359c4b51749d74dc0d2] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] claims to be a family member who has retained the right of residence or the family member or relative of an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] national he may not appeal under these Regulations unless he produces— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

- (a) [^key-b51c24656bc766ce96c231c5c651ea51]... ... [^key-b51c24656bc766ce96c231c5c651ea51]... a passport; and *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
- (b) either— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
  - (i) an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] family permit; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
  - (ia (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-7e82b3a7c2c2bd05f4e55e79125543f8][^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] a qualifying *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] State residence card; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
  - (ii (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] proof that he is the family member or relative of an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] national; or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
  - (iii (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] in the case of a person claiming to be a family member who has retained the right of residence, proof that he was a family member of the relevant person. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(3A)[^key-2b9cee18cd65fcfbd333ef5a0390f8ef] If a person claims to be a person with a derivative right of *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] entry or[^key-e6aef84e75c786ac0f80aadcdd79bda5] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] residence he may not appeal under these Regulations unless he produces a valid national identity card issued by an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] State or a passport, and either— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

- (b) proof that— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
  - (i) where the person claims to have *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] a derivative right of entry or residence as a result of[^key-78bb90c6c87404df9ac91d705acecb5a] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] regulation 15A(2), he is a direct relative or guardian of an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] national who is under the age of 18; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
  - (ii (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] where the person claims to have *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] a derivative right of entry or residence as a result of *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] regulation 15A(3), he is the child of an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] national; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
  - (iii (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] where the person claims to have *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] a derivative right of entry or residence as a result of *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] regulation 15A(4), he is a direct relative or guardian of the child of an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] national; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
  - (iv (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] where the person claims to have *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] a derivative right of entry or residence as a result of *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] regulation 15A(5), he is under the age of 18 and is a dependant of a person satisfying the criteria in *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] (i) or (iii);[^key-079e64e9fb60a2e37cfb2784e42e3e35] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
  - (v)[^key-4f0a9a7c57e1413cde989624a63e0789] where the person claims to have a derivative right of entry or residence as a result of regulation 15A(4A), he is a direct relative or guardian of a British citizen. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(4) A person may not bring an appeal under these Regulations on a ground certified under paragraph (5) or rely on such a ground in an appeal brought under these Regulations. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(5) The Secretary of State or an immigration officer may certify a ground for the purposes of paragraph (4) if it has been considered in a previous appeal brought under these Regulations or under section 82(1) of the 2002 Act *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] [^c1546088] .

(6) Except where an appeal lies to the Commission, an appeal under these Regulations lies to the *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] First-tier Tribunal[^key-f9353759d6b7c3a56534b0eede081d94] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794].

(8)[^key-c085f19b75dbc02e315a06e10f511abd] For the avoidance of doubt, nothing in this Part prevents a person who enjoys a right of appeal under this regulation from appealing to the First-tier Tribunal under section 82(1) of the 2002 Act (right of appeal to the Tribunal), or, where relevant, to the Commission pursuant to section 2 of the Special Immigration Appeals Act 1997 (jurisdiction of the Commission: appeals), provided the criteria for bringing such an appeal under those Acts are met. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

### 27. Out of country appeals (affected by repeal, see note)[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(1) Subject to paragraphs (2) and (3), a person may not appeal under regulation 26 whilst he is in the United Kingdom against an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] decision— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

- (a) to refuse to admit him to the United Kingdom; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
- (zaa (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-76f3632f62059f0cf0945295cf74b8be][^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] to revoke his admission to the United Kingdom; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
- (aa (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-0080cd68937e008cad98e9ada67670f9][^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] to make an exclusion order against him; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
- (b) to refuse to revoke a deportation *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] or exclusion[^key-de6d7f827e9b1d1a1a8899669dbc4fa8] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] order made against him; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
- (c) to refuse to issue him with an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] family permit; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
- (ca (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-76c26a555c54273f133dc95cb761e59a][^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] to revoke, or to refuse to issue or renew any document under these Regulations where that decision is taken at a time when the relevant person is outside the United Kingdom; or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
- (d)[^key-7c26d953e8f58cf941adefbcf5611360] to remove him from the United Kingdom after he has entered the United Kingdom in breach of a deportation or exclusion order *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] , or in circumstances where that person was not entitled to be admitted pursuant to regulation 19(1) or (1AB)[^key-7561a9cedaf5221a7922dd525199ddd5] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794].

(2) Paragraphs[^key-9cf4d2d267cf50dfcb4515aaac7758a9] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-c78141a065f9f1a2df9b7a4d8a25a99b] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] (1)(a) to (aa)[^key-f2df5e19965cc8664c88b18313928e6f] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-c78141a065f9f1a2df9b7a4d8a25a99b] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] do not apply where the person is in the United Kingdom and *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-c78141a065f9f1a2df9b7a4d8a25a99b] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]—

- (a) the person held *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] a valid[^key-145ce9ed3f7b68f53675ad60f4818489] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] family permit, registration certificate, residence card, *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] derivative residence card,[^key-757606426791bd972b59815cef5021bb] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] document certifying permanent residence *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] , permanent residence card or qualifying[^key-4ec707ce714b456891ca022995229f66] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] State residence card. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] on his arrival in the United Kingdom or can otherwise prove that he is resident in the United Kingdom *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] or[^key-1d3f5be130a41856c4d593dfa24306f5] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794];
- (b) the person is deemed not to have been admitted to the United Kingdom under regulation 22(3) but at the date on which notice of the decision to refuse to admit him is given he has been in the United Kingdom for at least 3 months *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] .[^key-17460d05e4d7fdf3d273b097d1e881b4] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] or[^key-1b23a9aa6d448f1bd905ef41f62a6b58] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
- (c)[^key-8e4d55b0aa906ae0ea3049ca04e48998] has made an asylum or human rights claim (or both), unless the Secretary of State has certified that the claim or claims is or are clearly unfounded. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(3)[^key-81aceefb50fe3c974dbf93ea87782be3] Paragraph (1)(d) does not apply where the person has made an asylum or human rights claim (or both), unless the Secretary of State has certified that the claim or claims is or are clearly unfounded. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-c78141a065f9f1a2df9b7a4d8a25a99b] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

### 28. Appeals to the Commission (affected by repeal, see note)[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(1) An appeal against an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] decision lies to the Commission where paragraph (2) or (4) applies. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(2) This paragraph applies if the Secretary of State certifies that the *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] decision was taken— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

- (a) by the Secretary of State wholly or partly on a ground listed in paragraph (3); or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
- (b) in accordance with a direction of the Secretary of State which identifies the person to whom the decision relates and which is given wholly or partly on a ground listed in paragraph (3). *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(3) The grounds mentioned in paragraph (2) are that the person's exclusion or removal from the United Kingdom is— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

- (a) in the interests of national security; or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
- (b) in the interests of the relationship between the United Kingdom and another country. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(4) This paragraph applies if the Secretary of State certifies that the *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] decision was taken wholly or partly in reliance on information which in his opinion should not be made public— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

- (a) in the interests of national security; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
- (b) in the interests of the relationship between the United Kingdom and another country; or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
- (c) otherwise in the public interest. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(5) In paragraphs (2) and (4) a reference to the Secretary of State is to the Secretary of State acting in person. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(6) Where a certificate is issued under paragraph (2) or (4) in respect of a pending appeal to the *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] First-tier Tribunal or Upper Tribunal[^key-e0645c76c845468fb3d1ff86f788c493] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] the appeal shall lapse. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(7) An appeal against an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] decision lies to the Commission where an appeal lapses by virtue of paragraph (6). *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(8) The Special Immigration Appeals Commission Act 1997 shall apply to an appeal to the Commission under these Regulations as it applies to an appeal under section 2 of that Act to which subsection (2) of that section applies (appeals against an immigration decision) but paragraph (i) of that subsection shall not apply in relation to such an appeal. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

### 28A. National security: (affected by repeal, see note) EEA (affected by repeal, see note) Decisions (affected by repeal, see note)[^key-6633db66af307322afb208c6b915545f][^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794][^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794][^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(1) Section 97A of the 2002 Act applies to an appeal against an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] decision where the Secretary of State has certified under regulation 28(2) or (4) that the *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] decision was taken in the interests of national security. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(2) Where section 97A so applies, it has effect as if— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

- (a) the references in that section to a deportation order were to an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] decision; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
- (b) subsections (1), (1A), (2)(b) and (4) were omitted; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
- (c) the reference in subsection (2)(a) to section 79 were a reference to regulations 27(2) and (3) and 29 of these Regulations; and *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
- (d) in subsection (2A), for sub-paragraphs (a) and (b), “against an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] decision” were substituted. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

### 29. Effect of appeals to the (affected by repeal, see note) First-tier Tribunal or Upper Tribunal (affected by repeal, see note)[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794][^key-08a785901c8ac049395243544a7bb728][^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(1) This Regulation applies to appeals under these Regulations made to the *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] First-tier Tribunal or Upper Tribunal[^key-ee0669664840483054e3828677215158] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794].

(2) If a person in the United Kingdom appeals against an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] decision to refuse to admit him to the United Kingdom *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] (other than a decision under regulation 19(1), (1A) or (1B))[^key-66769cfeabec2bb59eec22375096385f] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] any directions for his removal from the United Kingdom previously given by virtue of the refusal cease to have effect, except in so far as they have already been carried out, and no directions may be so given while the appeal is pending. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(3) If a person in the United Kingdom appeals against an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] decision to remove him from the United Kingdom *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] (other than a decision under regulation 19(3)(b))[^key-f1da1401add30a5f8ddc2d6a92dff4b8] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794], any directions given under section 10 of the 1999 Act or Schedule 3 to the 1971 Act for his removal from the United Kingdom are to have no effect, except in so far as they have already been carried out, while the appeal is pending. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(4) But the provisions of Part I of Schedule 2, or as the case may be, Schedule 3 to the 1971 Act with respect to detention and persons liable to detention apply to a person appealing against a refusal to admit him *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] , or a decision to revoke his admission,[^key-3637ee6f61b48acaec2b8fb66754e623] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] or a decision to remove him as if there were in force directions for his removal from the United Kingdom, except that he may not be detained on board a ship or aircraft so as to compel him to leave the United Kingdom while the appeal is pending. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(4A)[^key-8b394d331d1fd5dc17cb16006e662186] In paragraph (4), the words “except that he” to the end do not apply to an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] decision to which regulation 24AA applies. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(5) In calculating the period of two months limited by paragraph 8(2) of Schedule 2 to the 1971 Act for— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

- (a) the giving of directions under that paragraph for the removal of a person from the United Kingdom; and *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
- (b) the giving of a notice of intention to give such directions, *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

any period during which there is pending an appeal by him under is to be disregarded *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] (except in cases where the[^key-35a280de29259d6933f433435fe97c27] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] decision was taken pursuant to regulation 19(1), (1A), (1B) or (3)(b)) *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794].

(6) If a person in the United Kingdom appeals against an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] decision to remove him from the United Kingdom, a deportation order is not to be made against him under section 5 of the 1971 Act while the appeal is pending. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(7) Paragraph 29 of Schedule 2 to the 1971 Act (grant of bail pending appeal) applies to a person who has an appeal pending under these Regulations as it applies to a person who has an appeal pending under section 82(1) of the 2002 Act. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

### 29A. Alternative evidence of identity and nationality (affected by repeal, see note)[^key-c86948806810af6e29f4a807066b2740][^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(1) Subject to paragraph (2), where a provision of these Regulations requires a person to hold or produce a valid identity card issued by an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] State or a valid passport the Secretary of State may accept alternative evidence of identity and nationality where the person is unable to obtain or produce the required document due to circumstances beyond his or her control. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(2) This regulation does not apply to regulation 11. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

### 29AA (affected by repeal, see note). Temporary admission in order to submit case in person (affected by repeal, see note)[^key-93b11860106329c247466fcca5ac63ad][^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794][^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(1) This regulation applies where— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

- (a) a person (“P”) was removed from the United Kingdom pursuant to regulation 19(3)(b); *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
- (b) P has appealed against the decision referred to in sub-paragraph (a); *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
- (c) a date for P’s appeal has been set by the First Tier Tribunal or Upper Tribunal; and *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
- (d) P wants to make submissions before the First Tier Tribunal or Upper Tribunal in person. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(2) P may apply to the Secretary of State for permission to be temporarily admitted (within the meaning of paragraphs 21 to 24 of Schedule 2 to the 1971 Act, as applied by this regulation) to the United Kingdom in order to make submissions in person. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(3) The Secretary of State must grant P permission, except when P’s appearance may cause serious troubles to public policy or public security. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(4) When determining when P is entitled to be given permission, and the duration of P’s temporary admission should permission be granted, the Secretary of State must have regard to the dates upon which P will be required to make submissions in person. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(5) Where— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

- (a) P is temporarily admitted to the United Kingdom pursuant to this regulation; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
- (b) a hearing of P’s appeal has taken place; and *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
- (c) the appeal is not finally determined, *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

P may be removed from the United Kingdom pending the remaining stages of the redress procedure (but P may apply to return to the United Kingdom to make submissions in person during the remaining stages of the redress procedure in accordance with this regulation). *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(6) Where the Secretary of State grants P permission to be temporarily admitted to the United Kingdom under this regulation, upon such admission P is to be treated as if P were a person refused leave to enter under the 1971 Act for the purposes of paragraphs 8, 10, 10A, 11, 16 to 18 and 21 to 24 of Schedule 2 to the 1971 Act. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(7) Where Schedule 2 to the 1971 Act so applies, it has effect as if— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

- (a) the reference in paragraph 8(1) to leave to enter were a reference to admission to the United Kingdom under these Regulations; and *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
- (b) the reference in paragraph 16(1) to detention pending a decision regarding leave to enter or remain in the United Kingdom were to detention pending submission of P’s case in person in accordance with this regulation. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(8) P will be deemed not to have been admitted to the United Kingdom during any time during which P is temporarily admitted pursuant to this regulation. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

  - (ii) a “matter” in subsection (2) and a “new matter” in subsection (6) include a ground of appeal of a kind listed in section 84 of the 2002 Act and an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EU *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] ground of appeal; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

## SCHEDULE 2 (affected by repeal, see note) — EFFECT ON OTHER LEGISLATION (affected by repeal, see note)[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794][^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

Regulation 30 *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

### 1. Leave under the 1971 Act (affected by repeal, see note)[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(1) In accordance with section 7 of the Immigration Act 1988 *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] [^c1546091] , a person who is admitted to or acquires a right to reside in the United Kingdom under these Regulations shall not require leave to remain in the United Kingdom under the 1971 Act during any period in which he has a right to reside under these Regulations but such a person shall require leave to remain under the 1971 Act during any period in which he does not have such a right. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(2) Subject to sub-paragraph (3),[^key-9d098e738abae44e97d64f19b3d9706d] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] where a person has leave to enter or remain under the 1971 Act which is subject to conditions and that person also has a right to reside under these Regulations, those conditions shall not have effect for as long as the person has that right to reside. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(3)[^key-1320fcbe7e5063a3bcb7571eae35014f] Where the person mentioned in sub-paragraph (2) is an accession State national subject to worker authorisation working in the United Kingdom during the accession period and the document endorsed to show that the person has leave is an accession worker authorisation document, any conditions to which that leave is subject restricting his employment shall continue to apply. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(4) In sub-paragraph (3)—  *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

- (a) “ **accession period *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]** ” has the meaning *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] given in regulation 1(2)(c) of the Accession (Immigration and Worker Authorisation) Regulations 2006; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-1459eb6d15ca8b07f9452061d3f120fb] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]given in— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-1459eb6d15ca8b07f9452061d3f120fb] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
- (i) regulation 1(2)(c) of the Accession (Immigration and Worker Authorisation) Regulations 2006, in relation to a person who is an accession State national subject to worker authorisation within the meaning of regulation 2 of those Regulations; and *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-1459eb6d15ca8b07f9452061d3f120fb] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
- (ii (affected by repeal, see note) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-1459eb6d15ca8b07f9452061d3f120fb][^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] regulation 1(2) of the Accession of Croatia (Immigration and Worker Authorisation) Regulations 2013, in relation to a person who is an accession State national subject to worker authorisation within the meaning of regulation 2 of those Regulations; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-1459eb6d15ca8b07f9452061d3f120fb] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
- (b) “ **accession State national subject to worker authorisation *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]** ” has the meaning *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] given in regulation 2 of those Regulations; and *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-2488ee084efaf0ab27b37e9b5c7475e9] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]given in— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-2488ee084efaf0ab27b37e9b5c7475e9] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
- (i) regulation 2 of the Accession (Immigration and Worker Authorisation) Regulations 2006; and *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-2488ee084efaf0ab27b37e9b5c7475e9] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
- (ii (affected by repeal, see note) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-2488ee084efaf0ab27b37e9b5c7475e9][^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] regulation 2 of the Accession of Croatia (Immigration and Worker Authorisation) Regulations 2013; and *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-2488ee084efaf0ab27b37e9b5c7475e9] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
- (c) “ **accession worker authorisation document *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]** ” has the meaning *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] given in regulation 9(2) of those Regulations. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8ca7a3220589e1083694ba2922a712e3] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] given in— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8ca7a3220589e1083694ba2922a712e3] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
- (i) regulation 9(2) of the Accession (Immigration and Worker Authorisation) Regulations 2006, in relation to a person who is an accession State national subject to worker authorisation within the meaning of regulation 2 of those Regulations; and *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
- (ii (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] regulation 1(2) of the Accession of Croatia (Immigration and Worker Authorisation) Regulations 2013, in relation to a person who is an accession State national subject to worker authorisation within the meaning of regulation 2 of those Regulations. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

### 2. Persons not subject to restriction on the period for which they may remain (affected by repeal, see note)[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(1) For the purposes of the 1971 Act and the British Nationality Act 1981 *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] [^c1546092], a person who has a permanent right of residence under regulation 15 shall be regarded as a person who is in the United Kingdom without being subject under the immigration laws to any restriction on the period for which he may remain. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(2) But a qualified person, the family member of a qualified person *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] , a person with a derivative right of residence[^key-53efef4d69fbf4b96ab023e40fe80f79] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] and a family member who has retained the right of residence shall not, by virtue of that status, be so regarded for those purposes. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

### 3. Carriers' liability under the 1999 Act (affected by repeal, see note)[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

For the purposes of satisfying a requirement to produce a visa under section 40(1)(b) of the 1999 Act *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] [^c1546093] (charges in respect of passenger without proper documents), “ *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] **a visa of the required kind *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]** ” includes an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] family permit, a residence card *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794], a derivative residence card[^key-b5936bc22a8f65525fbf34f66503f8e7] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] , a qualifying[^key-929f7496c775a5b61afd1ed7e5585806] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] State residence card *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794], permission to be temporarily admitted under regulation 29AA[^key-f8df5adaaa2dc3c84e12668107872353] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] or a permanent residence card required for admission under regulation 11(2). *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

- (c) subsection (5) also applies where P does not have a right to reside in the United Kingdom under these Regulations, or only has such a right to reside by virtue of regulation 15B of these Regulations (continuation of a right of residence). *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

## SCHEDULE 4 (affected by repeal, see note) — TRANSITIONAL PROVISIONS (affected by repeal, see note)[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794][^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

### 1. Interpretation (affected by repeal, see note)[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

In this Schedule— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

- (a) the “ *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] **2000 Regulations *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]** ” means the Immigration (European Economic Area) Regulations 2000 *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] [^c1546099] and expressions used in relation to documents issued or applied for under those Regulations shall have the meaning given in regulation 2 of those Regulations; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
- (b) the “ *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] **Accession Regulations *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]** ” means the Accession (Immigration and Worker Registration) Regulations 2004 *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] [^c1546100] .

### 2. Existing documents (affected by repeal, see note)[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(1) An *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] family permit issued under the 2000 Regulations shall, after 29 *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] <sup>th *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]</sup> April 2006, be treated as if it were an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] family permit issued under these Regulations. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(2) Subject to paragraph (4), a residence permit issued under the 2000 Regulations shall, after 29 *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] <sup>th *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]</sup> April 2006, be treated as if it were a registration certificate issued under these Regulations. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(3) Subject to paragraph (5), a residence document issued under the 2000 Regulations shall, after 29 *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] <sup>th *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]</sup> April 2006, be treated as if it were a residence card issued under these Regulations. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(4) Where a residence permit issued under the 2000 Regulations has been endorsed under the immigration rules to show permission to remain in the United Kingdom indefinitely it shall, after 29 *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] <sup>th *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]</sup> April 2006, be treated as if it were a document certifying permanent residence issued under these Regulations and the holder of the permit shall be treated as a person with a permanent right of residence under regulation 15. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(5) Where a residence document issued under the 2000 Regulations has been endorsed under the immigration rules to show permission to remain in the United Kingdom indefinitely it shall, after 29 *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] <sup>th *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]</sup> April 2006, be treated as if it were a permanent residence card issued under these Regulations and the holder of the permit shall be treated as a person with a permanent right of residence under regulation 15. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(6) Paragraphs (4) and (5) shall also apply to a residence permit or residence document which is endorsed under the immigration rules on or after 30 *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] <sup>th *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]</sup> April 2006 to show permission to remain in the United Kingdom indefinitely pursuant to an application for such an endorsement made before that date. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

### 3. Outstanding applications (affected by repeal, see note)[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(1) An application for an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] family permit, a residence permit or a residence document made but not determined under the 2000 Regulations before 30 April 2006 shall be treated as an application under these Regulations for an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] family permit, a registration certificate or a residence card, respectively. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(2) But the following provisions of these Regulations shall not apply to the determination of an application mentioned in sub-paragraph (1)— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

- (a) the requirement to issue a registration certificate immediately under regulation 16(1); and *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
- (b) the requirement to issue a certificate of application for a residence card under regulation 17(3). *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

### 4. Decisions to remove under the 2000 Regulations (affected by repeal, see note)[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(1) A decision to remove a person under regulation 21(3)(a) of the 2000 Regulations shall, after 29 *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] <sup>th *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]</sup> April 2006, be treated as a decision to remove that person under regulation 19(3)(a) of these Regulations. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(2) A decision to remove a person under regulation 21(3)(b) of the 2000 Regulations, including a decision which is treated as a decision to remove a person under that regulation by virtue of regulation 6(3)(a) of the Accession Regulations, shall, after 29 *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] <sup>th *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]</sup> April 2006, be treated as a decision to remove that person under regulation 19(3)(b) of these Regulations. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(3) A deportation order made under section 5 of the 1971 Act by virtue of regulation 26(3) of the 2000 Regulations shall, after 29 *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] <sup>th *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]</sup> April 2006, be treated as a deportation made under section 5 of the 1971 Act by virtue of regulation 24(3) of these Regulations. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

### 5. Appeals (affected by repeal, see note)[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(1) Where an appeal against an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] decision under the 2000 Regulations is pending immediately before 30 *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] <sup>th *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]</sup> April 2006 that appeal shall be treated as a pending appeal against the corresponding *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] Decision under these Regulations. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(2) Where an appeal against an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] decision under the 2000 Regulations has been determined, withdrawn or abandoned it shall, on and after 30 *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] <sup>th *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]</sup> April 2006, be treated as an appeal against the corresponding *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] decision under these Regulations which has been determined, withdrawn or abandoned, respectively. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(3) For the purpose of this paragraph— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

- (a) a decision to refuse to admit a person under these Regulations corresponds to a decision to refuse to admit that person under the 2000 Regulations; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
- (b) a decision to remove a person under regulation 19(3)(a) of these Regulations corresponds to a decision to remove that person under regulation 21(3)(a) of the 2000 Regulations; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
- (c) a decision to remove a person under regulation 19(3)(b) of these Regulations corresponds to a decision to remove that person under regulation 21(3)(b) of the 2000 Regulations, including a decision which is treated as a decision to remove a person under regulation 21(3)(b) of the 2000 Regulations by virtue of regulation 6(3)(a) of the Accession Regulations; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
- (d) a decision to refuse to revoke a deportation order made against a person under these Regulations corresponds to a decision to refuse to revoke a deportation order made against that person under the 2000 Regulations, including a decision which is treated as a decision to refuse to revoke a deportation order under the 2000 Regulations by virtue of regulation 6(3)(b) of the Accession Regulations; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
- (e) a decision not to issue or renew or to revoke an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] family permit, a registration certificate or a residence card under these Regulations corresponds to a decision not to issue or renew or to revoke an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] family permit, a residence permit or a residence document under the 2000 Regulations, respectively. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

### 6. Periods of residence prior to the entry into force of these Regulations (affected by repeal, see note)[^key-6dd56c0b4cd37dd2d901a0352f7da69f][^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(1) Any period during which a person (“P”), who is an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] national, carried out an activity or was resident in the United Kingdom in accordance with the conditions in subparagraph (2) or (3) is to be treated as a period during which the person carried out that activity or was resident in the United Kingdom in accordance with these Regulations for the purpose of calculating periods of activity and residence there under. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(2) P carried out an activity, or was resident, in the United Kingdom in accordance with this subparagraph where such activity or residence was at that time in accordance with— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

- (a) the 2000 Regulations; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
- (b) the Immigration (European Economic Area) Order 1994 (“the 1994 Order”); or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
- (c) where such activity or residence preceded the entry into force of the 1994 Order, any of the following Directives which was at the relevant time in force in respect of the United Kingdom— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
  - (i) Council Directive *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] [64/221/ EEC *(affected by repeal, see note)*](https://www.legislation.gov.uk/european/regulation/1964/0221) [^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] ;
  - (ii (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] Council Directive *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] [68/360/ EEC *(affected by repeal, see note)*](https://www.legislation.gov.uk/european/regulation/1968/0360) [^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] ;
  - (iii (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] Council Directive *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] [72/194/ EEC *(affected by repeal, see note)*](https://www.legislation.gov.uk/european/regulation/1972/0194) [^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] ;
  - (iv (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] Council Directive *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] [73/148/ EEC *(affected by repeal, see note)*](https://www.legislation.gov.uk/european/regulation/1973/0148) [^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] ;
  - (v) Council Directive *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] [75/34/ EEC *(affected by repeal, see note)*](https://www.legislation.gov.uk/european/regulation/1975/0034) [^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] ;
  - (vi (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] Council Directive *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] [75/35/ EEC *(affected by repeal, see note)*](https://www.legislation.gov.uk/european/regulation/1975/0035) [^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] ;
  - (vii (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] Council Directive *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] [90/364/ EEC *(affected by repeal, see note)*](https://www.legislation.gov.uk/european/regulation/1990/0364) [^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] ;
  - (viii (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] Council Directive *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] [90/365/ EEC *(affected by repeal, see note)*](https://www.legislation.gov.uk/european/regulation/1990/0365) [^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] ; and *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
  - (ix (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] Council Directive *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] [93/96/ EEC *(affected by repeal, see note)*](https://www.legislation.gov.uk/european/regulation/1993/0096) [^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] .

(3) P carried out an activity or was resident in the United Kingdom in accordance with this subparagraph where P— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

- (a) had leave to enter or remain in the United Kingdom; and *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
- (b) would have been carrying out that activity or residing in the United Kingdom in accordance with these Regulations had the relevant state been an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] State at that time and had these Regulations at that time been in force. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(4) Any period during which P carried out an activity or was resident in the United Kingdom in accordance with subparagraph (2) or (3) will not be regarded as a period during which P carried out that activity or was resident in the United Kingdom in accordance with these Regulations where it was followed by a period— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

- (a) which exceeded two consecutive years and for the duration of which P was absent from the United Kingdom; or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
- (b) which exceeded two consecutive years and for the duration of which P’s residence in the United Kingdom— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
  - (i) was not in accordance with subparagraph (2) or (3); or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]
  - (ii (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] was not otherwise in accordance with these Regulations. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(5) The relevant state for the purpose of subparagraph (3) is the state of which P is, and was at the relevant time, a national. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

### The Asylum and Immigration Tribunal (Procedure) Rules 2005 (affected by repeal, see note)[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

**8.** (1) The Asylum and Immigration Tribunal (Procedure) Rules 2005 *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] [^c1546109] are amended as follows. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]

(2) In regulation 18(1)(b), after “(“ *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] **the 2000 Regulations *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]** ”)“ there is inserted “or, on or after 30 *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] <sup>th *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]</sup> April 2006, paragraph 4(2) of Schedule 2 to the Immigration (European Economic Area) Regulations 2006 (“ *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] **the 2006 Regulations *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794]** ”)“.

(3) In regulation 18(2), after “2000 Regulations” there is inserted *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] “ or paragraph 4(2) of Schedule 2 to the 2006 Regulations ” *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-696687a3596dec556ac133fa03ca8794] .

*(This note is not part of the Regulations)*

These Regulations implement Directive [2004/38/EC](https://www.legislation.gov.uk/european/directive/2004/0038) of the European Parliament and the Council of 29<sup>th</sup> April 2004 on the right of citizens of the Union and their family members to move and reside freely within the territory of the Member States amending Regulation [(EEC) No 1612/68](https://www.legislation.gov.uk/european/regulation/1968/1612) and repealing Directives [64/221/EEC](https://www.legislation.gov.uk/european/directive/1964/0221), [68/360/EEC](https://www.legislation.gov.uk/european/directive/1968/0360), [72/194/EEC](https://www.legislation.gov.uk/european/directive/1972/0194), [73/148/EEC](https://www.legislation.gov.uk/european/directive/1973/0148), [75/35/EEC](https://www.legislation.gov.uk/european/directive/1975/0035), [90/364/EEC](https://www.legislation.gov.uk/european/directive/1990/0364), [90/365/EEC](https://www.legislation.gov.uk/european/directive/1990/0365) and [93/96/EEC](https://www.legislation.gov.uk/european/directive/1993/0096). The Regulations come into force on 30<sup>th</sup> April 2006. A Transposition Note setting how the Government has transposed into UK law the main elements of this Directive will be available on the Office of Public Sector Information website.

Directive [2004/38/EC](https://www.legislation.gov.uk/european/directive/2004/0038) provides for the free movement of Union citizens and their family members within the territory of the member States. The repealed Directives were extended to Norway, Iceland and Liechtenstein by the Agreement on the European Economic Area signed at Oporto on 2<sup>nd</sup> May 1992 (OJ No. L 1, 3.1.94, p.3) and it is envisaged that Directive [2004/38/EC](https://www.legislation.gov.uk/european/directive/2004/0038) will also be extended to these States. In addition, an agreement between the European Community and its member States, of the one part, and the Swiss Confederation, of the other part, on the free movement of persons, signed at Brussels on 21<sup>st</sup> June 1999 (Cm 4904) confers on Swiss nationals and their family members broadly similar rights of entry into and residence in the United Kingdom as were contained in the repealed Directives. As was the case with the Regulations implementing the repealed Directives, these Regulations will also apply to nationals from Norway, Iceland Liechtenstein and Switzerland and their family members as well as to Union citizens and their family members. This will avoid having to apply a slightly different free movement regime to nationals from Norway, Iceland, Liechtenstein and Switzerland and their family members from that which has to apply to Union citizens and their family members under Directive [2004/38/EC](https://www.legislation.gov.uk/european/directive/2004/0038).

Directive [2004/38/EC](https://www.legislation.gov.uk/european/directive/2004/0038) is based on the provisions of the repealed Directives but it also contains new provisions, some of which reflect the case law of the European Court of Justice relating to the repealed Directives and the free movement of persons and some of which represent new developments of the law on the free movement of persons. The main new developments, which are reflected in these Regulations, are:

- (a) the inclusion of civil partners as family members of EU nationals along with spouses so far as member States who treat such partnerships as equivalent to marriage are concerned;
- (b) the introduction of an initial right of residence of 3 months in a host member State for EU nationals and their family members provided they do not become an unreasonable burden on the social assistance system of the host member State – this right of residence is not conditional on the EU national being, for example, a worker, self-employed, as was the case under the repealed Directives;
- (c) the introduction of a permanent right of residence in a host member State, which generally applies after 5 years residence in that member State.

Part 1 (regulations 1 to 10) of the Regulations contains the interpretation provisions for the Regulations. Part 2 (regulations 11 to 15) sets out the free movement rights conferred on EEA nationals—

- (i) the right of EEA nationals and their family members to be admitted to the United Kingdom provided they have the relevant documents (*regulation 11*);
- (ii) the right of EEA nationals and their family members to reside in the United Kingdom for an initial period of 3 months (*regulation 13*);
- (iii) the right of a “qualified person” (a jobseeker, worker, self-employed person, self-sufficient person or student), a family member a qualified person, and a “family member who has retained the right of residence” (for example, a family member of a deceased qualified person who satisfies specified conditions) to reside in the United Kingdom for as long as they have this status (*regulation 14*); and
- (iv) the right of EEA nationals and their family members to permanent residence in the United Kingdom in specified circumstances (for example, after they have resided in the United Kingdom under the Regulations for 5 years (*regulation 15*)).

Part 3 (regulations 16 to 18) provides for the issue of residence documentation, which can be used as proof of the rights of residence provided for in the Regulations. Part 4 (regulations 19 to 21) provides for the exclusion and removal of EEA nationals and their family members. As under the previous Directives, EEA nationals and their family members can be excluded on public policy, public security and public health grounds. Part 5 (regulations 22 to 24) contains procedural provisions relating to persons who claim admission under the Regulations, who are refused admission, or are being removed. Part 6 (regulations 25 to 29) and Schedule 1 set out the appeal rights in relation to decisions taken under the Regulations. Schedule 2 deals with the effect of the Regulations on other legislation. Schedule 3 lists the regulations that are being repealed by the new Regulations. Schedule 4 contains transitional provisions. Schedule 5 contains consequential amendments.
